\left\{X^{(i)}\right\}_{kj}=\frac{\exp\left\{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kj}\right\}}{\sum_{l=1}^{p}\exp\left\{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}\right\}},\quad\forall 1\leq k\leq n,1\leq j\leq p.

(
40
) implies

e^{-3}\bar{\nu}^{(i)}\leq\nu_{k}^{(i)}\leq e^{3}\bar{\nu}^{(i)},\frac{e^{-3}}{p}\leq X_{kl}^{(i)}\leq\frac{e^{3}}{p},\quad\forall 1\leq k\leq n,1\leq l\leq p.

(41)

Using the fact that
D_{KL}(W_{1},W_{2})=\lambda_{1}\log(\lambda_{1}/\lambda_{2})+\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1}
for
W_{1}\sim
Poisson(
\lambda_{1}
) and
W_{2}\sim
Poisson(
\lambda_{2}
), we have

\begin{split}D_{KL}\left(W^{(i)},W^{(j)}\right)=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\left[e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\right]\\
=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{s_{0}}\left[e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\right]\\
&+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=p-p_{0}+1}^{p-p_{0}+s_{0}}\left[e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\right].\end{split}

(42)

The second equation holds since
\exp\left(\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}\right)=\exp\left(\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}\right)=1
for all
1\leq k\leq n,l\notin\{1,\dots,s_{0}\}\cup\{p-p_{0}+1,\dots,p-p_{0}+s_{0}\}
.
For any
l\in\{1,2,\dots,s_{0}\}
,

\begin{split}&e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\\
=&e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\left[\exp\left\{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}-\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}\right\}-\left\{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}-\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}\right\}-1\right]\\
=&e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\left[\exp\left\{\left(\widetilde{V}^{(j)}\right)_{kl}-\left(\widetilde{V}^{(i)}\right)_{kl}\right\}-\left\{\left(\widetilde{V}^{(j)}\right)_{kl}-\left(\widetilde{V}^{(i)}\right)_{kl}\right\}-1\right]\\
=&e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\left[\exp\left\{\frac{\theta}{s_{0}}\left(\sum_{m\in\Omega_{j}}v_{km}^{\prime}-\sum_{m\in\Omega_{i}}v_{km}^{\prime}\right)\right\}-\frac{\theta}{s_{0}}\left(\sum_{m\in\Omega_{j}}v_{km}^{\prime}-\sum_{m\in\Omega_{i}}v_{km}^{\prime}\right)-1\right]\\
=&e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\left(e^{z_{k,i:j}}-z_{k,i:j}-1\right),\end{split}

(43)

where

v_{km}^{\prime}=v_{km}-v=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}1,&\text{with probability}1/2,\\
-1,&\text{with probability}1/2,\end{array}\right.

z_{k,i:j}=\frac{\theta}{s_{0}}\left(\sum_{m\in\Omega_{j}\backslash\Omega_{i}}v_{km}^{\prime}-\sum_{m\in\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}}v_{km}^{\prime}\right).

Noting that

|z_{k,i:j}|\leq\frac{\theta}{s_{0}}\left(\left|\Omega_{j}\backslash\Omega_{i}\right|+\left|\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}\right|\right)\leq\frac{\theta}{s_{0}}2s_{0}=2\theta\leq 2c\surd\left\{\frac{sp\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)}{n\bar{\nu}}\right\}\leq 2c\surd\left\{\frac{sp\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)}{Cs\log(p)\cdot p}\right\}<1,

we have

e^{z_{k,i:j}}-z_{k,i:j}-1\leq z_{k,i:j}^{2}.

(44)

Since
v_{km}^{\prime}
are i.i.d. Rademacher random variables, by Hoeffding’s inequality,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{m\in\Omega_{j}\backslash\Omega_{i}}v_{km}^{\prime}-\sum_{m\in\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}}v_{km}^{\prime}\right|>t\right)\leq 2\exp\left\{-\frac{2t^{2}}{4\left(\left|\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}\right|+\left|\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}\right|\right)}\right\}\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{4s_{0}}\right),

which means

{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{\left(\sum_{m\in\Omega_{j}\backslash\Omega_{i}}v_{km}^{\prime}-\sum_{m\in\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}}v_{km}^{\prime}\right)^{2}>t\right\}\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t}{4s_{0}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t}{s}\right).

For
q\geq 1
,

\begin{split}E\left|\left(\frac{s_{0}}{\theta}z_{k,i:j}\right)^{2}\right|^{q}=&E\left\{\left(\sum_{m\in\Omega_{j}\backslash\Omega_{i}}v_{km}^{\prime}-\sum_{m\in\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}}v_{km}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\right\}^{p}\\
=&\int_{0}^{\infty}qt^{q-1}{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{\left(\sum_{m\in\Omega_{j}\backslash\Omega_{i}}v_{km}^{\prime}-\sum_{m\in\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}}v_{km}^{\prime}\right)^{2}>t\right\}dt\\
\leq&\int_{0}^{\infty}qt^{q-1}\times 2\exp\left(-\frac{t}{s}\right)dt=2q!s^{q}\leq 2\left(\frac{q+1}{2}\right)^{q}s^{q}.\end{split}

Thus

\left\|\left(\frac{s_{0}}{\theta}z_{k,i:j}\right)^{2}\right\|_{\psi_{1}}=\sup_{q\geq 1}\left[\frac{1}{q}\left\{E\left|\left(\frac{s_{0}}{\theta}z_{k,i:j}\right)^{2}\right|^{q}\right\}^{\frac{1}{q}}\right]\leq\sup_{q\geq 1}\left(2^{1/q}\frac{q+1}{2q}s\right)\leq 2s.

Therefore

\|z_{k,i:j}^{2}-Ez_{k,i:j}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 2\|z_{k,i:j}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 2\frac{2\theta^{2}s}{s_{0}^{2}}\leq 2\frac{2\theta^{2}s}{\left\lfloor\frac{s}{4}\right\rfloor^{2}}\leq\frac{200\theta^{2}}{s}.

By Bernstein-type inequality,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}z_{k,i:j}^{2}-E\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}z_{k,i:j}^{2}\right)\right|\geq t\right\}\leq 2\exp\left\{-c^{\prime}\min\left(\frac{s^{2}t^{2}}{n\theta^{4}},\frac{st}{\theta^{2}}\right)\right\}.

(45)

Set
t=\frac{n\theta^{2}}{s_{0}}
in (
45
), since

E\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}z_{k,i:j}^{2}\right)=n\frac{\theta^{2}}{s_{0}^{2}}\left(\left|\Omega_{i}\backslash\Omega_{j}\right|+\left|\Omega_{j}\backslash\Omega_{i}\right|\right)\leq\frac{2n\theta^{2}}{s_{0}},

we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}z_{k,i:j}^{2}>3\frac{n\theta^{2}}{s_{0}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-c^{\prime}n\right).

(46)

In addition, by (
39
), for all
1\leq k\leq n,1\leq l\leq p
,

\begin{split}e^{v-\frac{3}{2}}\leq e^{v-1-\theta}\leq e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\leq e^{v+1+\theta}\leq e^{v+\frac{3}{2}}.\end{split}

(47)

(
43
), (
44
) and the previous inequality imply that

e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\leq e^{v+\frac{3}{2}}z_{k,i:j}^{2}.

for all
1\leq k\leq n,1\leq l\leq s_{0}
.
Thus

\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{s_{0}}\left[e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\right]\leq s_{0}\cdot e^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}z_{k,i:j}^{2},

By (
46
) and the previous inequality, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{s_{0}}\left[e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\right]>3ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\theta^{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-c^{\prime}n\right).

(48)

Similarly,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=p-p_{0}+1}^{p-p_{0}+s_{0}}\left[e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\log\left\{\frac{e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}}{e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}}\right\}+e^{\left(V^{(j)}\right)_{kl}}-e^{\left(V^{(i)}\right)_{kl}}\right]>3ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\theta^{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-c^{\prime}n\right).

(49)

By (
42
), (
48
) and (
49
),

{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{D_{KL}\left(W^{(i)},W^{(j)}\right)>6ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\theta^{2}\right\}\leq 4\exp\left(-c^{\prime}n\right).

Thus,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{\exists 1\leq i\neq j\leq M,D_{KL}\left(W^{(i)},W^{(j)}\right)>6ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\theta^{2}\right\}\leq 2M(M-1)\exp\left(-c^{\prime}n\right).

Since
\log\left(2M\left(M-1\right)\right)\asymp s\log(p/s)
and
n\geq Cs\log p
for sufficient large
C
, we know that

{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{\exists 1\leq i\neq j\leq M,D_{KL}\left(W^{(i)},W^{(j)}\right)>6ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\theta^{2}\right\}<\frac{1}{3},

which means

{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{\forall 1\leq i\neq j\leq M,D_{KL}\left(W^{(i)},W^{(j)}\right)\leq 6ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\theta^{2}\right\}>\frac{2}{3}.

(50)

By (
38
), (
41
) and (
50
), there exist fixed
\nu^{(i)}\in\mathbb{R}^{n},X^{(i)}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times p}
(
1\leq i\leq M
) satisfying
\bar{\nu}/e^{3}\leq\nu_{k}^{(i)}\leq e^{3}\bar{\nu}
and
e^{-3}p\leq X_{kl}\leq e^{3}p
,
\bar{V}^{(i)}=V^{(i)}P\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times p}
(
1\leq i\leq M
) satisfying RIP condition with constant
\delta_{2s}(\bar{V}^{(i)})=1/20
, and
\forall 1\leq i\neq j\leq M
,

D_{KL}\left(W^{(i)},W^{(j)}\right)\leq 6ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\theta^{2}=6ne^{v+\frac{3}{2}}\cdot c^{2}\frac{s\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)}{ne^{v}}=6e^{\frac{3}{2}}c^{2}s\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)\leq cs\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right).

(51)

Since
V^{(1)}\beta^{(1)}=\cdots=V^{(M)}\beta^{(n)}
,
y^{(i)}
and
y^{(j)}
have the same distribution, and
D_{KL}(y^{(i)},y^{(j)})=0.

Combine (
32
), (
51
) and the previous inequality,

D_{KL}\left((y^{i},W^{(i)}),(y^{(i)},W^{(j)})\right)\leq cs\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right).

Set
v=\log(Q/p)
, by (
47
) and
\nu_{kl}^{(i)}=e^{(V^{(i)})_{kl}}
,
e^{-3/2}Q\leq\bar{\nu}\leq e^{3/2}Q
. By (
34
) and (
47
),

\min_{i\neq j}\|\beta^{(i)}-\beta^{(j)}\|_{2}>\frac{1}{2}R\theta\geq c\left\{\frac{s\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)}{ne^{v}}\right\}^{1/2}R=c\left\{\frac{sp\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)}{nQ}\right\}^{1/2}R.

By the precious two inequalities and generalized Fano’s lemma, we have

\inf_{\hat{\beta}}\sup_{(\nu,X,\beta)\in\mathcal{F}_{p,n,s}(R,Q)}E\left(\|\hat{\beta}-\beta\|_{2}\right)\geq c\left\{\frac{sp\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)}{nQ}\right\}^{1/2}R\left\{1-\frac{cs\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)+\log 2}{\log M}\right\}.

Note that
\log M\asymp s\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)
, there exists a small constant
c>0
such that

\inf_{\hat{\beta}}\sup_{(\nu,X,\beta)\in\mathcal{F}_{p,n,s}(R,Q)}E\left(\|\hat{\beta}-\beta\|_{2}^{2}\right)\geq\left\{\inf_{\hat{\beta}}\sup_{(\nu,X,\beta)\in\mathcal{F}_{p,n,s}(R,Q)}E\left(\|\hat{\beta}-\beta\|_{2}\right)\right\}^{2}\geq c\frac{sp\log\left(\frac{p}{s}\right)}{nQ}R^{2}.

In summary, we have proved our assertion.

B.3
Proof of Theorem
3.4

W_{ij}
can be seen as
W_{ij}|q_{i}\sim\text{Poisson}(\nu_{i}q_{ij})
and
q_{ij}\sim\text{Beta}(\alpha_{i}X_{ij},\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{i}X_{ij})
. We introduce the truncated version of
W_{ij}
as

W^{\prime}_{ij}|W_{ij},q_{i}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}W_{ij},\quad q_{ij}\in\left[\frac{X_{ij}}{10},10X_{ij}\right]\text{and }W\in[\frac{\nu_{i}q_{ij}}{10},10\nu_{i}q_{ij}],\\
\nu_{ij},\quad\text{otherwise}.\end{array}\right.

Under the setting of Theorem
3.4
, we introduce the following lemmas.

Lemma B.6
.

\left|E\{\log(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i})\}-\log\left(\nu_{ij}\right)\right|\leq 10\zeta_{i}/\nu_{ij}
holds if
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)
.

Lemma B.7
.

\left|E\{\log^{2}(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i})\}-\log^{2}\left(\nu_{ij}\right)\right|\leq C\log(\nu_{ij})\zeta_{i}/\nu_{ij}
holds if
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)
.

Lemma B.8
(Sub-Gaussianity).

\left\|\surd{(\nu_{ij}/\zeta_{\max})}[\log\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}+z_{i}\right)-E\{\log(W^{\prime}_{ij}+z_{i})\}]\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq K_{0}
for a constant
K_{0}
.

Lemma B.9
(Infinity norm bound).

Under the setting of Theorem
3.4
, there exists constants
C,C^{\prime}>0
, such that

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\bar{A}_{W}\beta^{*}-\bar{B}_{W}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}\leq C\left[\surd{(n\log p)}\left\{\sigma+\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\zeta_{\max}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right\}+n\frac{\log(\bar{\nu}/p)}{\bar{\nu}/p}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right]\right)\geq 1-6p^{-C^{\prime}}.

Applying these lemmas, we can prove Theorem
3.4
by essentially the same method as the proof of Theorem
3.1
with
\lambda=C\left(\{\log(p)/n\}^{1/2}[\sigma+\{(p/\bar{\nu})\zeta_{\max}\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}]+\log(\bar{\nu}/p)(\bar{\nu}/p)^{-1}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)
.

B.4
Proof of Theorem
4.1

In the following lemmas, suppose
W_{ij}^{\prime}=W_{ij}1_{\left\{\nu_{ij}/10\leq W_{ij}\leq 10\nu_{ij}\right\}}+\nu_{ij}1_{\left\{W_{ij}\notin\left[\nu_{ij}/10,10\nu_{ij}\right]\right\}}
.

Lemma B.10
(Bias of
\log(W_{0}^{\prime}+1/2)
).

For any
\varepsilon>0
, there exists a constant
C_{\epsilon}>0
that only relies on
\epsilon
such that for all
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{\epsilon}
, we have
|E\log(W_{ij}^{\prime}+1/2)-\log\nu_{ij}|\leq\nu_{ij}^{-3/2+\epsilon}+F/(2\nu_{ij})
for some constant
C
.

Lemma B.11
(Bias of
\log^{2}(W_{0}^{\prime}+1/2)
).

There exists a constant
C>0
such that if
\nu_{ij}\geq C
, then
|E\{\log^{2}(W_{ij}^{\prime}+1/2)\}-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}|\leq F\log(\nu_{ij})/\nu_{ij}+C/\nu_{ij}
for some constant
C
.

Lemma B.12
(Sub-Gaussianity).

There exist positive constants
K_{0}
and
C
, such that for
\nu_{ij}\geq C
, we have

\left\|\nu_{ij}^{1/2}\left[\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+1/2\right)-E\left\{\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+1/2\right)\right\}\right]\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq K_{0}.

Lemma B.13
.

Given the setting in Theorem
4.1
, there exist two constants
C
and
C^{\prime}
, such that with probability at least
1-3p^{-C^{\prime}}
,

\left\|\bar{{A}}_{W}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}\leq C\left(n\log p\left(\sigma^{2}+\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+n^{2}s\left(\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{3-2\epsilon}+\min\{F^{2},C\}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{2}\right)\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

By these lemmas, we can prove Theorem
4.1
essentially the same as the proof of Theorem
3.1
.

\appendixthree

Appendix C
Technical Lemmas

We collect the technical lemmas used in the theoretical analysis in this section.

Lemma C.1
(Poisson tail bound (
Boucheron
et al. 2013
, Pages 22 - 23)).

For any
x\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W\geq v+x\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{x^{2}}{2v}\psi_{Benn}\left(x/v\right)\right).

For any
0\leq x\leq v
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W\leq v-x\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{x^{2}}{2v}\psi_{Benn}\left(-x/v\right)\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{x^{2}}{2v}\right),

where
\phi_{Benn}
is the Bennett function:
\psi_{Benn}(t)=\frac{(1+t)\log(1+t)-t}{t^{2}/2}
for
t>-1,t\neq 0
, and
\psi_{Benn}(0)=1
.

Lemma C.2
(Truncated Poisson tail bound).

There exists constant
c>0
such that for any
x\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq v+x\right)\leq\exp\left(-c\frac{x^{2}}{v}\right),

for any
0\leq x\leq\lambda
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\leq v-x\right)\leq\exp\left(-c\frac{x^{2}}{v}\right).

Proof of Lemma
C.2
.
By definition of
W^{\prime}
and Lemma
C.1
, for any
0\leq x\leq 9v
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq v+x\right)\leq{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W\geq v+x\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{x^{2}}{2v}\psi_{Benn}\left(x/v\right)\right).

Noting that
\psi_{Benn}
is a positive continuous function, denote
c>0
is a constant such that
\min_{0\leq t\leq 9}\psi_{Benn}(t)=2c
, then

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq v+x\right)\leq\exp\left(-c\frac{x^{2}}{v}\right).

For any
x>9v
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq v+x\right)=0<\exp\left(-c\frac{x^{2}}{v}\right).

Therefore, for any
x>0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\leq v-x\right)\leq{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W\leq v-x\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{x^{2}}{2v}\right).

For
x=0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\leq v-x\right)\leq 1=\exp\left(-\frac{x^{2}}{2v}\right).

Consider the regression model (
3
) and (
4
). Denote
V,\bar{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times p}
where
V_{ij}=\log(\nu_{i}X_{ij})
and
\bar{V}=V\{I_{p}-(1/p)1_{p}1_{p}^{\top}\}
. The following Lemma
C.3
shows that the RIP condition (Condition
3
) holds if the deterministic matrix
\bar{V}
satisfies RIP condition with constant
\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})<1/20
in the case without overdispersion.

Lemma C.3
(RIP condition).

Suppose
n\geq Cs\log(p)
,
\bar{\nu}\geq p
, and
a\bar{\nu}\leq\nu_{i}\leq b\bar{\nu}
,
a/p\leq X_{ij}\leq b/p
for constants
0<a<1<b
. If for some large constant
C>0
, some
\epsilon>0
, we have
\bar{\nu}\geq Cp(s+\log(np))
, and
\bar{V}
satisfies RIP condition with constant
\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})
, then with probability
1-4p^{-C^{\prime}}
,
\bar{{B}}_{W}
satisfies RIP condition with constants
\delta_{2s}(\bar{{B}}_{W})=2\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})
, i.e.,

n\left(1-2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq x^{\top}\bar{{A}}_{W}x=\|\bar{{B}}_{W}x\|_{2}^{2}\leq n\left(1+2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}

(52)

holds for all
2s
-sparse vector
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
, with probability
1-4p^{-C^{\prime}}
.

Proof of Lemma
C.3
.
Define
\|X\|_{\psi_{1}}=\sup_{p\geq 1}p^{-1}(E|X|^{p})^{1/p},\|X\|_{\psi_{2}}=\sup_{p\geq 1}p^{-1/2}(E|X|^{p})^{1/p}
.
For fixed
x
such that
\|x\|_{2}=1
, supp
(x)\subseteq S_{0}\subset\{1,\dots,p\}
and
\left|S_{0}\right|=2s
, denote
\widetilde{x}={P}x
,
\bar{x}=\frac{1}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}
. Then

\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(x_{i}-\bar{x}\right)^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}-p\bar{x}^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}=1.

(53)

First consider
x^{\top}\left(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)x
. Note that
{A}_{W^{\prime}}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}{A}_{W^{\prime},k}
, where

{A}_{W^{\prime},k}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p},\quad(A_{W^{\prime},k})_{ij}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj}),&i\neq j\\
\phi_{2}(W^{\prime}_{ki}),&i=j,\end{array}\right.

and
\phi_{1}(\cdot)=\log\left(\cdot+\frac{1}{2}\right),\phi_{2}(\cdot)=\log^{2}\left(\cdot+\frac{1}{2}\right)
,
we have

x^{\top}\left(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)x=\sum_{k=1}^{n}x^{\top}\left(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}\right)x,

where
\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k}={P}{A}_{W^{\prime},k}{P}
. If we can bound
\|x^{\top}\left(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}\right)x\|_{\psi_{1}}
, then Bernstein-type inequality could give us an upper bound for
{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|x^{\top}\left(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)x\right|\geq t\right)
.
Actually,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}(|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k})x|>t)={\mathrm{pr}}(|\widetilde{x}^{\top}({A}_{W^{\prime},k}-E{A}_{W^{\prime},k})\widetilde{x}|>t)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}(|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}((A_{W^{\prime},k})_{ij}-E(A_{W^{\prime},k})_{ij})\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}|>t).\end{split}

(54)

Denote
D_{ki}=\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)
, by Lemma
B.3
, there exist two constants
C
and
K_{0}>0
, if
\nu_{\min}\geq C
, then

\|\sqrt{\nu_{ki}}D_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq K_{0}.

Thus,

\|D_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\nu_{ki}}}K_{0}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}K_{0}.

(55)

In addition,

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\left(A_{W^{\prime},k}\right)_{ij}-E\left(A_{W^{\prime},k}\right)_{ij}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\\
=&\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq p,i\neq j}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\phi_{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-E\phi_{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(E\phi_{1}^{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-\left(E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\right)^{2}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\left(D_{ki}+E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\right)\left(D_{kj}+E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)-E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}D_{ki}D_{kj}\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}+2\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{ki}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}.\end{split}

(56)

Therefore,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\left(A_{W^{\prime},k}\right)_{ij}-E\left(A_{W^{\prime},k}\right)_{ij}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right|>t\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}D_{ki}D_{kj}\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|2\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{ki}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right).\end{split}

(57)

Denote

{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p},A_{ij}=\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j},D_{k}=\left(D_{k1},\dots,D_{kp}\right)^{\top},

then

\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}D_{ki}D_{kj}\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}=D_{k}^{\top}{A}D_{k},

and

\begin{split}ED_{k}^{\top}{A}D_{k}=&\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq p,i\neq j}\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}ED_{ki}ED_{kj}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\\
=&0+\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}.\end{split}

Denote
K=\frac{1}{\sqrt{C}}K_{0}
, (
55
) tells us

\max_{i\in S}\|D_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq K,\quad\forall\nu_{\min}\geq C.

By Hanson-Wright inequality, for every
t\geq 0
, we know that there exists a constant
c>0
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}D_{ki}D_{kj}\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left[-c\cdot\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{4K^{4}\|{A}\|_{HS}^{2}},\frac{t}{2\|{A}\|}\right)\right],\end{split}

where

\begin{split}\|{A}\|^{2}_{HS}&=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left|A_{ij}\right|^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{j}^{2},\quad\|{A}\|=\max_{\|u\|_{2}\leq 1}\|{A}u\|_{2}.\end{split}

By (
53
),

\|{A}\|^{2}_{HS}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\right)^{2}\leq 1,

and

\begin{split}&\|{A}\|=\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|u\|_{2}=1\\
\|v\|_{2}=1\end{subarray}}\left|u^{T}{A}v\right|=\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|u\|_{2}=1\\
\|v\|_{2}=1\end{subarray}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}u_{i}A_{ij}v_{j}\right|\leq\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|u\|_{2}=1\\
\|v\|_{2}=1\end{subarray}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left|u_{i}\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}v_{j}\right|\\
\leq&\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|u\|_{2}=1\\
\|v\|_{2}=1\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left|u_{i}\widetilde{x}_{i}\right|\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left|v_{j}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right|\right)\leq\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|u\|_{2}=1\\
\|v\|_{2}=1\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}u_{i}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}v_{i}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq 1.\end{split}

(58)

The third inequality of (
58
) comes from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

Thus,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}D_{ki}D_{kj}\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}ED_{ki}^{2}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{4K^{4}},\frac{t}{2}\right)\right].\end{split}

(59)

Now we consider
{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|2\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{ki}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)
.
Since

\frac{a}{b}\nu_{ki^{\prime}}\leq\nu_{ki}\leq\frac{b}{a}\nu_{ki^{\prime}},\quad\forall 1\leq i,i^{\prime}\leq p,

we have

\log\nu_{ki}\leq\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\log\nu_{ki^{\prime}},\quad\forall 1\leq i,i^{\prime}\leq p.

By Lemma
B.1
, there exists a constant
C>0
, for
\nu_{\min}\geq C
,

|E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\log\nu_{ki}|\leq\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}},\quad\forall 1\leq i,i^{\prime}\leq p,1\leq k\leq n.

Therefore, if
\nu_{\min}\geq C
,

\left|E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki^{\prime}}\right)\right|\leq\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}},\quad\forall 1\leq i,i^{\prime}\leq p.

Moreover,

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right)^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\widetilde{x}_{j}\right)^{2}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\left(x_{j}-\bar{x}\right)\right)^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\overline{E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{k\cdot})}\right)x_{j}\right)^{2}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\left(\sum_{j\in S_{0}}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\overline{E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{k\cdot})}\right)x_{j}\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\sum_{j\in S_{0}}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\overline{E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{k\cdot})}\right)^{2}\sum_{j\in S_{0}}x_{j}^{2}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\sum_{j\in S_{0}}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\overline{E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{k\cdot})}\right)^{2},\end{split}

where
\overline{E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{k\cdot})}=\frac{1}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)
. The inequality comes from Cauchy-Schwarz Inequality.
Since

\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\overline{E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{k\cdot})}\right)^{2}\leq\max_{i^{\prime},i}\left|E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki^{\prime}}\right)\right|^{2}\leq\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{2},

for
\nu_{\min}\geq C
,

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widetilde{x}_{i}^{2}\cdot 2s\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{2}\leq 2s\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{C}\right)^{2}.\end{split}

(60)

Denote
K_{1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}K_{0}
, due to (
55
), (
60
) and Hoeffding-type inequality,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|2\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{ki}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)\\
\leq&e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{\left(\|\max_{i}D_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i}\widetilde{x}_{j}\right)^{2}}\right)\\
\leq&e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{sK_{1}^{2}}\right).\end{split}

(
54
), (
57
), (
59
) and the previous inequality together imply that

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|x^{\top}(\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k})x\right|>t\right)\leq 2\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{4K^{4}},\frac{t}{2}\right)\right]+e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{sK_{1}^{2}}\right).

For
q\geq 1
, we have

\begin{split}&E\left|x^{\top}(\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k})x\right|^{q}\\
=&\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|x^{\top}(\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k})x\right|>t\right)qt^{q-1}dt\\
\leq&\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(2\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{4K^{4}},\frac{t}{2}\right)\right]+e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{sK_{1}^{2}}\right)\right)qt^{q-1}dt\\
\leq&2\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{4K^{4}}\right)qt^{q-1}dt+e\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{sK_{1}^{2}}\right)qt^{q-1}dt\\
&+2\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{ct}{2}\right)qt^{q-1}dt\\
=&q\left(\frac{4K^{4}}{c}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\Gamma\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)+\frac{eq}{2}\left(\frac{sK_{1}^{2}}{c}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\Gamma\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)+2\left(\frac{2}{c}\right)^{q}q!\\
\leq&q\left(\frac{4K^{4}}{c}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}+\frac{eq}{2}\left(\frac{sK_{1}^{2}}{c}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}+2\left(\frac{2}{c}\right)^{q}\left(\frac{q+1}{2}\right)^{q}.\end{split}

Note that

x^{q}+y^{q}+z^{q}\leq(x+y+z)^{q},\quad\forall q\geq 1,x,y,z\geq 0,

for any
q\geq 1
,

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{q}\left(E\left|x^{\top}(\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{A}_{W^{\prime},k})x\right|^{q}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{q}\left(q^{\frac{1}{q}}\left(\frac{4K^{4}}{c}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\frac{eq}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\left(\frac{sK_{1}^{2}}{c}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+2^{\frac{1}{q}}\frac{q+1}{c}\right)\\
\leq&\frac{4K^{2}}{\sqrt{2c}}+eK_{1}\sqrt{\frac{s}{2c}}+\frac{4}{c}.\end{split}

The first inequality holds since
q^{\frac{1}{q}}q^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq 2q
and
2^{\frac{1}{q}}\frac{q+1}{q}\leq 2\times 2=4
for
q\geq 1
.

Thus for any
1\leq k\leq n
,

\|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k})x\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\frac{4K^{2}}{\sqrt{2c}}+eK_{1}\sqrt{\frac{s}{2c}}+\frac{4}{c}.

(61)

Note that
\nu_{\min}\gtrsim\bar{\nu}/p\geq Cs
,
K_{1}\sqrt{s}\leq C\frac{K_{0}}{\sqrt{s}}\sqrt{s}=CK_{0}
can be bounded by a constant. Therefore
for any
1\leq k\leq n
,

\|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k})x\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq C.

can be bounded by a constant.
By Bernstein-type inequality,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq t\right)=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime},k})x\right|\geq t\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left[-c\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{n},t\right\}\right].\end{split}

Thus there exists a constant
C>0
such that for
1\leq t\lesssim n
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq C\sqrt{nt}\right)\leq 2e^{-t},

which means there exists a constant
c>0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq\frac{1}{4}\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)n\right)\leq 2e^{-cn}.

(62)

For any
S_{0}\subset\{1,\dots,p\},|S_{0}|=2s
, by Lemma
C.7
with
\widetilde{{A}}=\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}
and
\epsilon=\frac{1}{4}
, we have

\sup_{x\in\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}}\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\leq 2\sup_{x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{1}{4}}}\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|,

(63)

where
\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}=\{x:x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\|x\|_{2}=1,\text{supp}(x)\subset S_{0}\}
and
\mathcal{N}_{\frac{1}{4}}
is an
\epsilon
-net of
\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}
. By Lemma 5.2 in
Vershynin 2010
, we can choose
\mathcal{N}_{\frac{1}{4}}
such that card
\left(\mathcal{N}_{\frac{1}{4}}\right)\leq 9^{2s}
. By (
62
),

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sup_{x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{1}{4}}}\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq\frac{1}{4}\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)n\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{1}{4}},\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq\frac{1}{4}\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)n\right)\\
\leq&9^{2s}\times 2e^{-cn}.\end{split}

(64)

Combine (
63
) and (
64
) together, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sup_{x\in\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}}\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq\frac{1}{2}\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)n\right)\leq 9^{2s}\times 2e^{-cn}.

Therefore,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\|x\|_{0}\leq s,\|x\|_{2}=1,\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq\frac{1}{2}\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)n\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists S_{0}\subset\{1,2,\dots,p\},\left|S_{0}\right|=2s,\sup_{x\in\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}}\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq\frac{1}{2}\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)n\right)\\
\leq&\binom{p}{2s}\times 9^{2s}\times 2e^{-cn}\leq e^{2s\log p}\times 9^{2s}\times 2e^{-cn}.\end{split}

(65)

Next, we need to bound
x^{\top}(E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-\bar{V}^{\top}\bar{V})x
.

By Lemma
B.1
and Lemma
B.2
,
\exists C_{\epsilon^{\prime}}>0
(only depends on
\epsilon^{\prime}
), if
\nu_{\min}\geq C_{\epsilon^{\prime}}\geq C
, then

\begin{split}\left(\log(\nu_{ki})-\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}\right)\left(\log(\nu_{kj})-\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}\right)&\leq E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\\
&\leq\left(\log(\nu_{ki})+\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}\right)\left(\log(\nu_{kj})+\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}\right),\end{split}

and

\log^{2}(\nu_{ki})-\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}}\leq E\phi_{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\leq\log^{2}(\nu_{ki})+\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}}.

Thus

\left|E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\log(\nu_{ki})\log(\nu_{kj})\right|\leq 8\frac{\log\nu_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\frac{16}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon^{\prime}}}.

Since
\nu_{\max}\asymp\nu_{\min}
, for
\nu_{\min}\geq C
,

\left|E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\log(\nu_{ki})\log(\nu_{kj})\right|\leq C^{\prime}\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\frac{16}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon^{\prime}}}.

\left|E\phi_{2}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\log^{2}\nu_{ki}\right|\leq\frac{4}{\nu_{\min}}.

Hence,

\begin{split}\max_{i,j}\left|\left(E{A}_{W^{\prime}}-V^{\top}V\right)_{ij}\right|=&\max_{i,j}\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})-\log\nu_{ki}\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\\
\leq&n\cdot\max\left\{C^{\prime}\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\frac{16}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon^{\prime}}},\frac{4}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}.\end{split}

(66)

Also, for any
p\times p
matrix
{A}
, one can show that

\max_{i,j}\left|\left({P}^{\top}{A}{P}\right)_{ij}\right|\leq 4\max_{i,j}\left|A_{ij}\right|.

(67)

In fact,

\begin{split}\left|\left({P}^{\top}{A}{P}\right)_{ij}\right|=&\left|A_{ij}-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{p}A_{i^{\prime}j}-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{p}A_{ij^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{p}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{p}A_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}\right|\\
\leq&\left|A_{ij}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{p}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{p}A_{i^{\prime}j}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{p}A_{ij^{\prime}}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{p}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{p}A_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}\right|\\
\leq&4\max_{i,j}\left|A_{ij}\right|.\end{split}

Combine (
66
) and (
67
) together, we have

\begin{split}\left|x^{\top}(E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-\bar{V}^{\top}\bar{V})x\right|=&\left|\sum_{i\in S_{0}}\sum_{j\in S_{0}}x_{i}\left({P}\left(E{A}_{W^{\prime}}-V^{\top}V\right){P}\right)_{ij}x_{j}\right|\\
\leq&\sum_{i\in S_{0}}\sum_{j\in S_{0}}\left|x_{i}\right|\left|\left({P}\left(E{A}_{W^{\prime}}-V^{\top}\ V\right){P}\right)_{ij}\right|\left|x_{j}\right|\\
\leq&4\max_{i,j}\left|\left(E{A}_{W^{\prime}}-V^{\top}V\right)_{ij}\right|\sum_{i\in S_{0}}\sum_{j\in S_{0}}\left|x_{i}\right|\left|x_{j}\right|\\
\leq&4\max_{i,j}\left|\left(E{A}_{W^{\prime}}-V^{\top}V\right)_{ij}\right|\left(\sum_{i\in S_{0}}|x_{i}|\right)^{2}\\
\leq&4\max_{i,j}\left|\left(E{A}_{W^{\prime}}-V^{\top}V\right)_{ij}\right|\cdot 2s\sum_{i\in S_{0}}|x_{i}|^{2}\\
=&8s\cdot n\cdot\max\left\{C^{\prime}\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon^{\prime}}}+\frac{16}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon^{\prime}}},\frac{4}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}.\end{split}

(68)

Set
\epsilon^{\prime}=\frac{1}{5}
, and note that
\lim_{t\to\infty}\frac{\log t}{t^{\frac{1}{4}}}=0
, we know that there exists two constants
C
and
C^{\prime}
such that for any
\nu_{\min}\geq C
,

\left|x^{\top}(E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-\bar{V}^{\top}\bar{V})x\right|\leq\frac{C^{\prime}sn}{\nu_{\min}}.

Since
\nu_{\min}\gtrsim\bar{\nu}/p\geq Cs
, for any
2s
-sparse
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
such that
\|x\|_{2}=1
, we have

\left|x^{\top}(E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-\bar{V}^{\top}\bar{V})x\right|\leq\frac{1}{2}n\cdot\delta\left(\mathbf{\Gamma}\right).

(69)

Combine (
65
) and (
69
) together, also note that
\bar{V}^{\top}\bar{V}
satisfies RIP condition with constant
\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})
, we know that

n\left(1-2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq x^{\top}\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}x\leq n\left(1+2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}

(70)

holds for all
2s
-sparse
x
with probability at least
1-2\times 9^{2s}e^{2s\log p-cn}
.

By (
81
) and (
82
),

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\leq\frac{1}{10}\nu_{ij}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\geq 10\nu_{ij}\right)\leq 2e^{-c\nu_{ij}}.

(71)

(
70
) and (
71
) tell us

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall 2s\text{-sparse }x,n\left(1-2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq x^{\top}\bar{{A}}_{W}x\leq n\left(1+2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
\geq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall 2s\text{-sparse }x,n\left(1-2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq x^{\top}\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}x\leq n\left(1+2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
&-{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq n,1\leq j\leq p,W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\\
\geq&1-2\times 9^{2s}e^{2s\log p-cn}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{p}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\\
\geq&1-2\times 9^{2s}e^{2s\log p-cn}-2npe^{-c\nu_{\min}}.\end{split}

Since
\bar{\nu}\geq Cp\log(np)
, we know that
\nu_{\min}\geq C\log(np)
with a large constant
C
. Also notice that
n\geq Cs\log p
, there exists a large constant
C^{\prime}>0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall s\text{-sparse }x,n\left(1-2\delta\left(\Gamma\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq x^{\top}\bar{{A}}_{W}x\leq n\left(1+2\delta\left(\Gamma\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\geq 1-4p^{-C^{\prime}}.

Lemma C.4
(Truncated Beta tail bound).

Suppose
q\sim\text{Beta}(\alpha,\beta)
, define

q^{\prime}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}q,&\frac{\alpha}{10\left(\alpha+\beta\right)}\leq q\leq\frac{10\alpha}{\alpha+\beta},\\
\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta},&\text{otherwise}.\end{array}\right.

If
\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\asymp p
, then
\exists c>0
,
\forall t\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q^{\prime}\geq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}+t\right)\vee{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q^{\prime}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}-t\right)\leq 2e^{-cp(\alpha+\beta)t^{2}}.

Proof of Lemma
C.4
.
Since
\beta>\alpha
, by Theorem 9 in
Zhang and Zhou 2020
, for any
0<t<\frac{\beta}{\alpha+\beta}
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q\geq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}+t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-c\min\left\{\frac{\beta^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha},\beta t\right\}\right).

If
\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\asymp p
and
0<t\leq 9\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}
,

p(\alpha+\beta)t^{2}\asymp\frac{\beta^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha}\gtrsim\beta t.

Thus for any
t\in[0,9\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}]
,
\exists c>0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q\geq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}+t\right)\leq 2e^{-cp(\alpha+\beta)t^{2}}.

By the definition of
q^{\prime}
,
\forall t\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q^{\prime}\geq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}+t\right)\leq 2e^{-cp(\alpha+\beta)t^{2}}.

Moreover, apply Theorem 9 in
Zhang and Zhou 2020
, for any
0<t<\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q\leq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}-t\right)\leq 2e^{-c\frac{\beta^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha}}.

If
\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\asymp p
, then
\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha}\asymp p(\alpha+\beta)
, thus
\exists c>0
such that for all
t\in[0,\frac{9\alpha}{10\left(\alpha+\beta\right)}]
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q\leq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}-t\right)\leq 2e^{-cp\left(\alpha+\beta\right)t^{2}}.

Thus for any
t\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q^{\prime}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}-t\right)\leq 2e^{-cp\left(\alpha+\beta\right)t^{2}}.

Lemma C.5
(Truncated Beta-Poisson tail bound).

W|q\sim\text{Poisson}(\mu q)
, and
q\sim\text{Beta}(\alpha,\beta)
, define

W^{\prime}|W,q=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}W,&q\in\left[\frac{\alpha}{10\left(\alpha+\beta\right)},\frac{10\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right]\text{and }W\in[\frac{\mu q}{10},10\mu q],\\
\mu\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta},&\text{otherwise}.\end{array}\right.

If
\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\asymp p
, then we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}+t\right)\vee{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\leq\mu\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}-t\right)\leq 3e^{-ct^{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha+\beta}{\mu\alpha},\frac{(\alpha+\beta)^{2}}{\mu^{2}\alpha}\right\}}.\end{split}

Proof of Lemma
C.5
.

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right)=E\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)\right)\\
=&\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)\frac{1}{\text{B}(\alpha,\beta)}q^{a-1}(1-q)^{b-1}dq\\
=&\int_{0}^{\min\{(1+\frac{\delta}{2})\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta},1\}}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)\frac{1}{\text{B}(\alpha,\beta)}q^{a-1}(1-q)^{b-1}dq\\
&+\int_{\min\{(1+\frac{\delta}{2})\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta},1\}}^{1}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)\frac{1}{\text{B}(\alpha,\beta)}q^{a-1}(1-q)^{b-1}dq.\end{split}

By the definition of
W^{\prime}
, for
q\geq 10\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}
, we know that

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)=0.

Therefore,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right)\leq\sup_{q\in(0,(1+\frac{\delta}{2})\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}]}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q^{\prime}\geq\left(1+\frac{\delta}{2}\right)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right).\end{split}

(72)

By Lemma
C.2
, for any
t\geq 0
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(q+t)\right|q)\leq\exp\left(-c\frac{(\mu t)^{2}}{\mu q}\right).\end{split}

Hence, for all
q\in(0,(1+\frac{\delta}{2})\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}]
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)\leq&\exp\left(-c\frac{\left(\mu\left((1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}-q\right)\right)^{2}}{\mu q}\right)\\
\leq&\exp\left(-c\mu\frac{\delta^{2}}{1+\frac{\delta}{2}}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right).\end{split}

If
\delta\leq 99,\exists c>0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)\leq\exp\left(-c\mu\delta^{2}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right).

By the definition of
W^{\prime}
, for
\delta\geq 99
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu\left(1+\delta\right)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)=0.

Thus for all
\delta>0
and
q\in(0,(1+\frac{\delta}{2})\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}]
.

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\bigg|q\right)\leq\exp\left(-c\mu\delta^{2}\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right).

(73)

In addition, by Lemma
C.4
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q^{\prime}\geq\left(1+\frac{\delta}{2}\right)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right)\leq 2e^{-c\delta^{2}\alpha}.

Combine (
72
), (
73
) and the previous inequality together,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu(1+\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right)\leq 3e^{-c\alpha\delta^{2}\min\left\{1,\frac{\mu}{\alpha+\beta}\right\}}.\end{split}

Similarly, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\leq\mu(1-\delta)\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right)\leq 3e^{-c\alpha\delta^{2}\min\left\{1,\frac{\mu}{\alpha+\beta}\right\}}.\end{split}

Thus

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\geq\mu\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}+t\right)\leq 3e^{-ct^{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha+\beta}{\mu\alpha},\frac{(\alpha+\beta)^{2}}{\mu^{2}\alpha}\right\}},\\
&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}\leq\mu\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}-t\right)\leq 3e^{-ct^{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha+\beta}{\mu\alpha},\frac{(\alpha+\beta)^{2}}{\mu^{2}\alpha}\right\}}.\end{split}

Proof of Lemma
B.1
. In the following proof,
C_{i}\left(\epsilon\right)
and
C_{\epsilon}
only depend on
\epsilon
, and for fixed
\epsilon
, they are large constant.

According to the Lagrange form of Taylor’s Theorem,
\forall x>-1
,

\log(x+1)=x-\frac{x^{2}}{2}+\frac{x^{3}}{3(1+\xi)^{3}}

for some number
\xi
between 0 and
x
. Take
x=(t-v+\frac{1}{2})/v
, we know that

\log\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)=\log v+\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}-\frac{\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}{2v^{2}}+\frac{\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{3}}{3(1+\xi)^{3}v^{3}}

for some number
\xi
between 0 and
(t-v+\frac{1}{2})/v
.

Denote
f(t)=\log(t+\frac{1}{2})-\log v-\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}+\frac{(t-v+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}{2v^{2}}
,
\exists C_{1}(\epsilon_{1})>0
, if
v\geq C_{1}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right)
, for any
v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}<t<v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}
,

\left|f(t)\right|\leq v^{-\frac{3}{2}+3\epsilon_{1}}.

Therefore for all
v\geq C_{1}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right)
,

E\left|f(W_{0})1_{\{v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}<W_{0}<v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right|\leq v^{-\frac{3}{2}+3\epsilon_{1}}.

(74)

If
t\leq v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}},v\geq 1
, then

\begin{split}\left|f(t)\right|\leq&\max\left\{\log\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right),\log v\right\}+\left|\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}\right|+\left|\frac{\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}{2v^{2}}\right|\leq\log v+\frac{3}{2}.\end{split}

Set
x=v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}
in Lemma
C.1
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{v^{2\epsilon_{1}}}{2}\right).

Therefore,
\exists C_{2}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right)>0
, for
v\geq C_{2}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right)
,

\begin{split}E\left(\left|f(W_{0})1_{\{W_{0}\leq v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right|\right)\leq&\left(\log v+\frac{3}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\right)\\
\leq&\left(\log v+\frac{3}{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{v^{2\epsilon_{1}}}{2}\right)\leq v^{-\frac{3}{2}}.\end{split}

(75)

\forall t>v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}},v\geq 1
,

\begin{split}&\left|\log\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\log v-\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}+\frac{\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}{2v^{2}}\right|\\
\leq&\left|\log\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\log v\right|+\left|\frac{\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}{2v^{2}}-\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}\right|\\
=&\left|\log\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\log v\right|+\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}\left|\frac{t+\frac{1}{2}}{2v}-\frac{3}{2}\right|\\
\leq&\log\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)+\frac{3\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}{2v^{2}}\leq\log\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)+3\frac{t^{2}}{v^{2}}.\end{split}

(76)

Set
x=v^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon_{1}}
in Lemma
C.1
, since
\lim_{y\to 0}\psi_{Benn}(y)=1
, for
v\geq C_{3}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right)
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\left(v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\right)^{2}}{2v}\psi_{Benn}\left(\frac{v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}}{v}\right)\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{v^{2\epsilon_{1}}}{4}\right).

(77)

By the previous inequality and (
76
), for
v\geq C_{3}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right)
,

\begin{split}&\left|Ef(W_{0})1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right|\\
\leq&E\left(\log\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right)+3E\left(\frac{W^{2}}{v^{2}}1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right)\\
\leq&E\left(W_{0}1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right)+\frac{3}{v^{2}}E\left(W_{0}^{2}1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right)\\
\leq&\left[EW_{0}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{3}{v^{2}}\left[EW_{0}^{4}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\left(2v^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{v^{2\epsilon_{1}}}{8}\right)+\frac{3}{v^{2}}\left(2v^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{v^{2\epsilon_{1}}}{8}\right)\leq v^{-\frac{3}{2}}.\end{split}

(78)

The third inequality comes from Cauchy-Schwarz Inequality.

Set
\epsilon_{1}=\frac{\epsilon}{3}
, combine (
74
), (
75
) and (
78
) together, for
\nu\geq C_{\epsilon}=\max\{C_{1}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right),C_{2}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right),C_{3}\left(\epsilon_{1}\right)\}
,

\begin{split}\left|Ef(W_{0})\right|=&E\left|f(W_{0})1_{\{v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}<W_{0}<v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right|\\
&+\left|Ef(W_{0})1_{\{W_{0}\leq v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}\}}\right|+\left|Ef(W_{0})1_{W_{0}\geq v+v^{1/2+\epsilon_{1}}}\right|\\
\leq&v^{-\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}+v^{-\frac{3}{2}}+v^{-\frac{3}{2}}\\
\leq&3v^{-\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}.\end{split}

(79)

Note that
Ef(W_{0})=E\log(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2})-\log v+\frac{1}{8v^{2}}
, for
v\geq C_{\epsilon}
,

|E\log(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2})-\log v|\leq 3v^{-\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}+\frac{1}{8v^{2}}.

(80)

Moreover, by Lemma
C.1
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq v-\frac{9}{10}v\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\frac{9}{10}v\right)^{2}}{v}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{81}{100}v\right),

(81)

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq 10v\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{(9v)^{2}}{v}\psi_{Benn}\left(\frac{9v}{v}\right)\right)=\exp\left(-9\psi_{Benn}(9)v\right).

(82)

By the previous two inequalities, there exist constants
C,c>0
, for
v\geq C_{\epsilon}>C
,

\begin{split}&\left|E\log\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-E\log\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\left|\log\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\right)-E\log\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\}}\right|\\
&+\left|\log\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq 10v\right)-E\log\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\geq 10v\}}\right|\\
\leq&\max\left\{\log\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\right),E\left|\log\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right|1_{\{W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\}}\right\}\\
&+\max\left\{\log\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq 10v\right),E\log\left|\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right|1_{\{W_{0}\geq 10v\}}\right\}\\
\leq&\max\left\{\log\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right)e^{-cv},\left(E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\\
&+\max\left\{\log\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right)e^{-cv},\left(E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq 10v\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\\
\leq&2\max\left\{\log\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right)e^{-cv},\left(EW_{0}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-cv}\right\}\leq 2\sqrt{v}e^{-cv}.\end{split}

(83)

The third inequality is due to Cauchy-Schwarz Inequality.

(
80
) and the previous inequality imply that for
v\geq C_{\epsilon}
,

\begin{split}\left|E\log(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-\log v\right|\leq&\left|E\log(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2})-\log v\right|+\left|E\log(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2})-E\log(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2})\right|\\
\leq&3v^{-\frac{3}{2}+3\epsilon_{1}}+\frac{1}{8v^{2}}+2\sqrt{v}e^{-cv}\\
\leq&4v^{-\frac{3}{2}+3\epsilon_{1}}.\end{split}

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.
By Taylor’s expansion,

\log^{2}(v+x)=\log^{2}v+2\frac{\log v}{v}x+\frac{1-\log v}{v^{2}}x^{2}+\frac{2\log(v+\xi)-3}{3(v+\xi)^{3}}x^{3},

where
\xi
is a real number between 0 and
x
.

Let
x=t-v+\frac{1}{2}
, we have

\begin{split}\log^{2}\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)=&\log^{2}v+2\frac{\log v}{v}\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)+\frac{1-\log v}{v^{2}}\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}\\
&+\frac{2\log(v+\xi)-3}{3(v+\xi)^{3}}\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{3},\end{split}

where
\xi
is a real number between 0 and
t-v+\frac{1}{2}
.

Let
g(t)=\log^{2}(t+\frac{1}{2})-\log^{2}v-2\frac{\log v}{v}(t-v+\frac{1}{2})-\frac{1-\log v}{v^{2}}(t-v+\frac{1}{2})^{2}
, then there exists a constant
C>0
, for all
v-v^{5/8}<t<v+v^{5/8}
,
v\geq C
, we have

\begin{split}|g(t)|\leq&\frac{2\log(v+\xi)}{3(v+\xi)^{3}}\left|t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right|^{3}\leq\frac{2\log(3v)}{3\left(v-v^{5/8}\right)^{3}}\left|v^{5/8}+\frac{1}{2}\right|^{3}\leq\frac{\log v}{v^{3}}v^{15/8}\\
\leq&\frac{v^{\frac{1}{8}}}{v^{3}}v^{15/8}=\frac{1}{v}.\end{split}

Therefore, for
v\geq C
,

\left|Eg(W_{0})1_{\{v-v^{5/8}<W_{0}<v+v^{5/8}\}}\right|\leq\frac{1}{v}.

(84)

For
t\leq v-v^{5/8}
,
v\geq 1
, we have

\begin{split}\left|g(t)\right|\leq&\max\{\log^{2}\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right),\log^{2}v\}+2\log v\left|\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}\right|+\left|\frac{1-\log v}{v^{2}}\right|\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\log^{2}v+2\log v+\frac{\log v}{v^{2}}\left(v-\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}\leq\log^{2}v+2\log v+\log v=\log^{2}v+3\log v.\end{split}

By Lemma
C.1
, for
v\geq C
,

\begin{split}E(\left|g(W_{0})1_{\{W_{0}\leq v-v^{1/2+\epsilon_{2}}\}}\right|)\leq&\left(\log^{2}v+3\log v\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq v-v^{5/8}\right)\\
\leq&\left(\log^{2}v+3\log v\right)\exp\left(-\frac{v^{1/4}}{2}\right)\leq v^{-\frac{3}{2}}.\end{split}

(85)

\forall t>v+v^{5/8}
and
v\geq C
,

\begin{split}g(t)\leq&\left|\log^{2}\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\log^{2}v\right|+\left|2\frac{\log v}{v}\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)+\frac{1-\log v}{v^{2}}\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}\right|\\
\leq&\log^{2}\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)+\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}\left|2\log v+\frac{1-\log v}{v}\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\log^{2}\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)+\frac{t-v+\frac{1}{2}}{v}\left(2\log v+\frac{\log v}{v}\left(t-v+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)\\
\leq&\log^{2}\left(t+\frac{1}{2}\right)+2(\log v)\frac{t}{v}+(\log v)\frac{t^{2}}{v^{2}}\leq 3t+(\log v)\frac{t^{2}}{v^{2}}.\end{split}

(86)

By Lemma
C.1
, for all
v\geq C
with a large constant
C
,

{\mathrm{pr}}(W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8})\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\left(v^{\frac{5}{8}}\right)^{2}}{v}\psi_{Benn}\left(\frac{v^{\frac{5}{8}}}{v}\right)\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}v^{\frac{1}{4}}\right).

Combine (
86
) and the previous inequality together, for all
v\geq C
,

\begin{split}&\left|Eg(W_{0})1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8}\}}\right|\\
\leq&E\left(3W_{0}+(\log v)\frac{W_{0}^{2}}{v^{2}}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8}\}}\\
=&3EW_{0}1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8}\}}+\frac{\log v}{v^{2}}EW_{0}^{2}1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8}\}}\\
\leq&3\left(EW_{0}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\log v}{v^{2}}\left(EW_{0}^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&3\left(2v^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}v^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)+\frac{\log v}{v^{2}}\left(2v^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}v^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)\\
\leq&(6v+2\log v)\exp\left(-\frac{v^{1/4}}{4}\right)\leq v^{-\frac{3}{2}}.\end{split}

(87)

By (
84
), (
85
) and (
87
), for
v\geq C
, we have

\begin{split}\left|Eg(W_{0})\right|=&E\left|g(W_{0})1_{\{v-v^{5/8}<W_{0}<v+v^{5/8}\}}\right|\\
&+\left|Eg(W_{0})1_{\{W_{0}\leq v-v^{5/8}\}}\right|+\left|Eg(W_{0})1_{\{W_{0}\geq v+v^{5/8}\}}\right|\\
\leq&v^{-1}+v^{-\frac{3}{2}}+v^{-\frac{3}{2}}=\frac{1}{v}+\frac{2}{v^{\frac{3}{2}}}.\end{split}

(88)

Note that
Eg(W_{0})=E\log^{2}(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2})-\log^{2}v-\frac{1}{v}+\frac{\log v-1}{4v^{2}}
, for
v\geq C
,

\left|E\log^{2}(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2})-\log^{2}v\right|\leq\left|Eg(W_{0})\right|+\left|\frac{1}{v}-\frac{\log v-1}{4v^{2}}\right|\leq\frac{1}{v}+\frac{2}{v^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\max\{\frac{1}{v},\frac{\log v-1}{4v^{2}}\}=\frac{2}{v}+\frac{2}{v^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

(89)

Similarly to (
83
), there exists a constant
c>0
, for all
v\geq C
,

\begin{split}&\left|E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right|\\
=&\left|\log^{2}\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\right)-E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\}}\right|\\
&+\left|\log^{2}\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq 10v\right)-E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\geq 10v\}}\right|\\
\leq&\max\left\{\log^{2}\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\right),E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\}}\right\}\\
&+\max\left\{\log^{2}\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq 10v\right),E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)1_{\{W_{0}\geq 10v\}}\right\}\\
\leq&\max\left\{\log^{2}\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right)e^{-cv},\left(E\log^{4}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\leq\frac{1}{10}v\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\\
&+\max\left\{\log^{2}\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right)e^{-cv},\left(E\log^{4}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}\geq 10v\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\\
\leq&2\max\left\{\log^{2}\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right)e^{-cv},\left(EW_{0}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-cv}\right\}\\
\leq&2\sqrt{v}e^{-cv}.\end{split}

(90)

By (
89
) and the previous inequality,

\begin{split}&\left|E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\log^{2}v\right|\\
\leq&\left|E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-E\log^{2}\left(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right|+\left|E\log^{2}(W_{0}+\frac{1}{2})-\log^{2}v\right|\\
\leq&\frac{2}{v}+\frac{2}{v^{\frac{3}{2}}}+2\sqrt{v}e^{-cv}\leq\frac{4}{v}.\end{split}

Proof of Lemma
B.3
. For convenience, denote
D=\phi_{1}(W_{0}^{\prime})-E\phi_{1}(W_{0}^{\prime})
. By Lemma
B.1
, there exists a constant
C_{1}>0
such that
\left|E\phi_{1}(W_{0}^{\prime})-\log v\right|\leq\frac{4}{v}
. For
t>0
and
v\geq C_{1}
, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sqrt{v}D\right|>t\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sqrt{v}D>t\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sqrt{v}D<-t\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)>E\log\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)+\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)<E\log\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)-\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)>\log v-\frac{4}{v}+\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W_{0}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)<\log v+\frac{4}{v}-\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}>ve^{\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}-\frac{4}{v}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}+\frac{4}{v}}-\frac{1}{2}\right).\end{split}

(91)

\forall t\geq 1
and
v\geq C_{2}=\max\{C_{1},100\}
,

\begin{split}&v\left(e^{\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}-\frac{4}{v}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}\geq v\left(e^{\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}-\frac{4}{v}}-1-\frac{1}{2v}\right)\geq v\left(1+\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}-\frac{4}{v}-1-\frac{1}{2v}\right)\\
\geq&v\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}+v\left(\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}-\frac{9}{2v}\right)\geq v\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}>0.\end{split}

(92)

In the second inequality, we used
e^{x}\geq 1+x
for all
x\in\mathbb{R}
.

By Lemma
C.2
and (
92
),

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}>ve^{\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}-\frac{4}{v}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}>v+v\left(e^{\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}-\frac{4}{v}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}\right)\\
\leq&\exp\left(-c\frac{\left(v\left(e^{\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}-\frac{4}{v}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}}{v}\right)\leq\exp\left(-c\frac{\left(v\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}\right)^{2}}{v}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}ct^{2}\right).\end{split}

(93)

Now consider the left tail. For any
v\geq C_{2}
and
t\geq 1
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}+\frac{4}{v}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)\leq{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}-\frac{1}{2}\right).\end{split}

(94)

For any
t\geq 6\sqrt{v}
, we have
ve^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}-\frac{1}{2}<\frac{1}{10}v
, by the definition of
W_{0}^{\prime}
, immediately we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}+\frac{4}{v}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)=0.\end{split}

(95)

For any
t<6\sqrt{v}
, by Lemma
C.1
, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)\leq{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<v-v\left(1-e^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}\right)\right)\\
\leq&\exp\left(-\frac{\left(v\left(1-e^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}\right)\right)^{2}}{2v}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{v}{2}\left(1-e^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}\right)^{2}\right).\end{split}

(96)

Note that for
x>0
,

\left(\frac{1-e^{-x}}{x}\right)^{\prime}=\frac{(x+1)e^{-x}-1}{x^{2}}<0,

therefore for
t<6\sqrt{v},v\geq C_{2}
,
1-e^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}\geq\frac{1-e^{-3}}{3}\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}
, which means that

\begin{split}&\exp\left(-\frac{v}{2}\left(1-e^{-\frac{t}{2\sqrt{v}}}\right)^{2}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{v}{2}\frac{(1-e^{-3})^{2}}{9}\frac{t^{2}}{4v}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{100}t^{2}\right).\end{split}

(
94
), (
96
) and the previous inequality imply that for any
v\geq C_{2},t<6\sqrt{v}
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}+\frac{4}{v}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{100}t^{2}\right).

By (
95
) and the previous inequality, if
v\geq C_{2}
, then for all
t\geq 1
, we have

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{0}^{\prime}<ve^{-\frac{t}{\sqrt{v}}+\frac{4}{v}}-\frac{1}{2}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{100}t^{2}\right).\end{split}

(97)

combine (
91
), (
93
) and (
97
), we know that
\exists C,c>0
, for all
t\geq 1
,
v\geq C
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sqrt{v}D\right|>t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-ct^{2}\right).\end{split}

For
p\geq 1
, we have

\begin{split}&E\left|\sqrt{v}D\right|^{p}=\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sqrt{v}D\right|>t\right)pt^{p-1}dt\leq\int_{0}^{1}pt^{p-1}dt+\int_{1}^{\infty}2\exp\left(-ct^{2}\right)pt^{p-1}dt\\
\leq&1+\int_{0}^{\infty}2\exp\left(-ct^{2}\right)pt^{p-1}dt=1+c^{-\frac{p}{2}}p\Gamma\left(\frac{p}{2}\right)\leq 1+pc^{-\frac{p}{2}}\left(\frac{p}{2}\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}\\
\leq&\left(1+p^{\frac{1}{p}}c^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{p}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{p}.\end{split}

In the last step, we use
x^{p}+y^{p}\leq(x+y)^{p}
for all
x,y\geq 0,p\geq 1
.

Therefore, for
p\geq 1
, we have

\begin{split}&p^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(E\left|\sqrt{v}D\right|^{p}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq p^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+p^{\frac{1}{p}}c^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{p}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\leq 1+\frac{e^{\frac{1}{e}}c^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2}},\end{split}

which means

\left\|\sqrt{v}D\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq K_{0},

where
K_{0}=1+\frac{e^{\frac{1}{e}}c^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\sqrt{2}}
.

Proof of Lemma
B.4
. Since

\begin{split}y_{i}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\log\left(X_{ij}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}+\varepsilon_{i}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\log\left(X_{ij}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}+\log\nu_{i}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\beta_{j}^{*}+\varepsilon_{i}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\log\left(\nu_{ij}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}+\varepsilon_{i},\end{split}

i.e.,
y=V\beta^{*}+\varepsilon
(where
V_{ij}=\log\nu_{ij}
). Also note that
{P}\beta^{*}=\beta^{*}
,

\begin{split}&\left\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}=\left\|{P}{A}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-{P}{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}(V\beta^{*}+\varepsilon)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\left\|{P}({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}+\|\bar{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}.\\
\end{split}

More specifically,

\left(\bar{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\right)_{i}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\right)\varepsilon_{k}.

For any
t\geq 0
, by Hoeffding-type inequality,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t\Big|W^{\prime}_{kj},1\leq k\leq n,1\leq j\leq p\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}}}.

(98)

Denote
D_{ki}=\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki}),Q_{ki}=D_{ki}^{2}-ED_{ki}^{2},M_{ki}=\left(\bar{{B}}_{W}\right)_{ki}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W}\right)_{ki}
,
R_{ki}=M_{ki}^{2}-EM_{ki}^{2}
, we have shown that there exists a constant
K
such that
\|D_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq K
in Lemma
B.3
. According to Lemma 5.9 in
Vershynin 2010
,

\begin{split}\|M_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}=&\left\|(1-\frac{1}{p})D_{ki}+\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j\neq i}D_{kj}\right\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{p}\right)^{2}\|D_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{j\neq i}\|D_{kj}\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\right)\\
\leq&C\max_{j}\|D_{kj}\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\leq CK^{2}.\end{split}

(99)

Thus
\|M_{ki}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 2\|M_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\leq CK^{2}
, which leads to
\|R_{ki}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 2\|M_{ki}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq CK^{2}
. Note that

\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}=R_{ki}+2\left(E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)M_{ki},

\forall t\geq 0
, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}-\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\right|>t\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}R_{ki}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)M_{ki}\right|>\frac{t}{4}\right).\end{split}

(100)

By Bernstein-type inequality, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}R_{ki}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{nK^{4}},\frac{t}{K^{2}}\right)\right],

By Hoeffding-type inequality,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)M_{ki}\right|>\frac{t}{4}\right)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{K^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}}\right),

Set
t=n
, combine (
100
) and the previous two inequalities together, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}>\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}+n\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}-\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\right|>n\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}R_{ki}\right|>\frac{n}{2}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)M_{ki}\right|>\frac{n}{4}\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{n}{K^{4}},\frac{n}{K^{2}}\right)\right]+e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{cn^{2}}{K^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}}\right).\end{split}

(101)

By Lemma
B.2
,
E\phi_{1}^{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)=E\phi_{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\leq\log^{2}\nu_{ki}+\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}}
for all
\nu_{ki}\geq C
. Since
a\bar{\nu}\leq\nu_{k}\leq b\bar{\nu}
and
a/p\leq X_{ki}\leq b/p
, we know that
\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\nu_{ki}\leq\nu_{\min}
for all
1\leq k\leq n,1\leq i\leq p
. Thus
c\log^{2}\nu_{\min}\leq E\phi_{1}^{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\leq C\log^{2}\nu_{\min}
.

If
\nu_{\min}\geq C
, Lemma
B.1
and the entry-wise upper and lower bounds of
\nu_{i}
and
X_{ij}
together imply that

\begin{split}&\left|E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right|=\left|E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\left|\log\nu_{ki}-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\log\nu_{kj}\right|+\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}}+\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\frac{4}{\nu_{ki}}\leq\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}.\end{split}

(102)

By the previous inequality and the independence of
W^{\prime}_{kj}(j=1,2,\dots,p)
, for
\nu_{\min}\geq C
, we have

\begin{split}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}=&\left(E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)^{2}+\Var\left(\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)\\
\leq&\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{2}+\Var\left(\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\right)\\
\leq&\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{2}+\left(1-\frac{1}{p}\right)^{2}\Var\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\right)+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{j\neq i}\Var\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right).\end{split}

(103)

Notice that for all
\nu_{kj}\geq C
,

\begin{split}\Var\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)=&E\phi_{1}^{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\left(E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\log^{2}(\nu_{kj})+\frac{4}{\nu_{kj}}-\left(\log(\nu_{kj})-\frac{4}{\nu_{kj}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{4}}}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{8}{\nu_{kj}}.\end{split}

(104)

Combine (
103
) and the previous inequality, if
\nu_{\min}\geq C
, then

\begin{split}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\leq&\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{2}+\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{p}\right)^{2}+p\cdot\frac{1}{p^{2}}\right)\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\\
\leq&\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{2}+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\leq\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{C}\right)^{2}+\frac{8}{C}\end{split}

can be bounded by a constant. Thus

\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\leq Cn.

Therefore, if
\nu_{\min}\geq C
,
\forall t\geq 0
, by (
98
), (
101
) and the previous inequality, there exists an absolute constant
c>0
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}+n\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}>\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}+n\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\leq Cn\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}>\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}+n\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}\right)+(2+e)\exp\left(-cn\right).\end{split}

Thus

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}>t\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq p,\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\right)_{i}>t\right)\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t\right)\\
\leq&p\left(2e^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}+(2+e)e^{-cn}\right)\leq 5p\left(e^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}+e^{-cn}\right).\end{split}

(105)

Now we consider
\|{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\|_{\infty}
. Note that

({B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V)_{ij}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\log\nu_{kj},

we have

\begin{split}\left[\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}=&\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)_{ij}\beta_{j}^{*}\\
=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\\
=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})\sum_{j\in S}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*},\end{split}

where
S
is the support set of
\beta^{*}
.

Thus

\begin{split}\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kl}\right)\right)\sum_{j\in S}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\\
=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}.\end{split}

(106)

Consider
\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}
first.
By Lemma
B.1
,
\forall 1>\epsilon>0
,
\exists C_{\epsilon}\geq C>0
(only depends on
\epsilon
) such that for all
k,j
, if
\nu_{\min}\geq C_{\epsilon}
,

\left|E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right|\leq\frac{4}{\nu_{kj}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}}\leq\frac{4}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}}.

Apply Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}&\left(\sum_{j\in S}E\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{j\in S}\left(E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{16s}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2},\end{split}

i.e.,

\left|\sum_{j\in S}E\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\leq\frac{4\sqrt{s}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}.

(107)

For convenience, denote
U_{kj}=\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}
. Note that
\forall t\geq 0
, if
|XY-EXEY|>t
, then for any
\alpha>0
, we have
|X-EX|>\min\left(\frac{t}{3|EY|},\sqrt{\frac{\alpha t}{3}}\right)
or
|Y-EY|>\min\left(\frac{t}{3|EX|},\sqrt{\frac{t}{3\alpha}}\right)
. Otherwise

\begin{split}\left|XY-EXEY\right|=&\left|(X-EX)(Y-EY)+EY(X-EX)+EX(Y-EY)\right|\\
\leq&\left|(X-EX)(Y-EY)\right|+\left|EY(X-EX)\right|+\left|EX(Y-EY)\right|\\
\leq&\sqrt{\frac{\alpha t}{3}}\sqrt{\frac{t}{3\alpha}}+\left|EX\right|\frac{t}{3|EX|}+\left|EY\right|\frac{t}{3|EY|}=t.\end{split}

Contradiction! By (
102
) and (
107
), for any
t\geq 0
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right|>t\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right|>\min\left(\frac{t}{3\left|E\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|},\sqrt{\frac{t}{3\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}}\right)\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right|>\min\left(\frac{t}{3\left|E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right|},\sqrt{\frac{\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}t}{3}}\right)\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|M_{ki}\right|>\min\left(\frac{t}{\frac{12\sqrt{s}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}},\sqrt{\frac{t}{3\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}}\right)\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{j\in S}D_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|>\min\left(\frac{t}{3\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{24}{\nu_{\min}}},\sqrt{\frac{\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}t}{3}}\right)\right).\end{split}

(108)

By Lemma
B.3
and (
99
),

\|D_{kj}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\frac{K_{0}}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}},\quad\|M_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\leq C\max_{j}\|D_{kj}\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\leq C\frac{K_{0}^{2}}{\nu_{\min}}.

Due to Hoeffding-type inequality,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|M_{ki}\right|>\min\left(\frac{t}{\frac{12}{\nu_{\min}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}},\sqrt{\frac{t}{3\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}}\right)\right)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{c}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}}\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{s}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}},\frac{t}{\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}\right)\right).

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{j\in S}D_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|>\min\left(\frac{t}{6\frac{b}{a}+\frac{24}{\nu_{\min}}},\sqrt{\frac{\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}t}{3}}\right)\right)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{c}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\min\left(t^{2},\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}t\right)\right).

Combine (
108
) and the previous two inequalities together, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right|>t\right)\\
\leq&e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{c}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}}\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{s}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}},\frac{t}{\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}\right)\right)+e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{c}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\min\left(t^{2},\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}t\right)\right).\end{split}

For
q\geq 1
, the previous inequality implies that

\begin{split}&E\left|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right|^{q}\\
=&\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right|>t\right)qt^{q-1}dt\\
\leq&\int_{0}^{\infty}e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}\frac{s}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)qt^{q-1}dt+\int_{0}^{\infty}e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}\right)qt^{q-1}dt\\
&+\int_{0}^{\infty}e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct^{2}}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)qt^{q-1}dt+\int_{0}^{\infty}e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{ct}{\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}\right)qt^{q-1}dt\\
=&\frac{eq}{2}\Gamma\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)\left(\frac{K_{0}\sqrt{s}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{2-\epsilon}}\right)^{q}+2e\cdot\left(\frac{K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{c\nu_{\min}}\right)^{q}q!+\frac{eq}{2}\Gamma\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)\left(\frac{K_{0}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)^{q}\\
\leq&\frac{eq}{2}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\left(\frac{K_{0}\sqrt{s}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{2-\epsilon}}\right)^{q}+2e\cdot\left(\frac{K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{c\nu_{\min}}\right)^{q}\left(\frac{q+1}{2}\right)^{q}+\frac{eq}{2}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\left(\frac{K_{0}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)^{q}.\end{split}

Thus

\begin{split}&\left(E\left|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right|^{q}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\\
\leq&\left(\frac{eq}{2}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\left(\frac{K_{0}\sqrt{s}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{2-\epsilon}}\right)^{q}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\left(2e\cdot\left(\frac{K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{c\nu_{\min}}\right)^{q}\left(\frac{q+1}{2}\right)^{q}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\\
&+\left(\frac{eq}{2}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{q}{2}}\left(\frac{K_{0}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)^{q}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\\
\leq&q\left(2\frac{K_{0}\sqrt{s}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{2-\epsilon}}+2e\cdot\frac{K_{0}^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{c\nu_{\min}}+2\frac{K_{0}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{c}\nu_{\min}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right).\end{split}

The second inequality holds since
\left(2e\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\frac{q+1}{2}\leq 2eq
and
\left(\frac{eq}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\left(\frac{q}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{e}{2\sqrt{2}}\cdot 2q\leq 2q
for
q\geq 1
.
Set
\epsilon=\frac{1}{2}
in the previous inequality, we know that if
\nu_{\min}\geq Cs
,

\left\|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}.

Consider the centering, we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\psi_{1}}\\
\leq&2\left\|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\end{split}

for all
1\leq k\leq n
. By Bernstein-type inequality,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}-E\left(\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right)\right|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left[-c\min\left\{\frac{\nu_{\min}t^{2}}{n\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}},\frac{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}t}{\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}}\right\}\right].

Recall that
\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}
, set
t=C\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\sqrt{x}
with
1\leq x\lesssim n
and large constant
C
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}-E\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq\sqrt{n}\frac{C}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\sqrt{x}\right)\leq e^{-x}.

(109)

Since
\left|\Cov\left(X,Y\right)\right|\leq\sqrt{\Var\left(X\right)\Var\left(Y\right)}
, we have
