\begin{split}&\left|E\left[\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right]-E\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\\
\leq&\left(\Var\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)\Var\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

By (
104
), if
\nu_{\min}\geq C
,

\begin{split}&\Var\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\Var\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\\
=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{p}\right)^{2}\Var\left(\phi_{1}(W_{ki}^{\prime})\right)+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{j\neq i}\Var\left(\phi_{1}(W_{kj}^{\prime})\right)\right]\leq\frac{8n}{\nu_{\min}}.\end{split}

\Var\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)=\sum_{j\in S}\beta_{j}^{*2}\Var\left(U_{kj}\right)=\sum_{j\in S}\beta_{j}^{*2}\Var\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)\leq\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}.

Hence

\left|E\left[\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right]-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\leq\frac{8\sqrt{n}}{\nu_{\min}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}.

(110)

By (
106
), (
107
) and (
110
),

\begin{split}\left|E\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|=&\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left(\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\left|E\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}E\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|+\frac{8\sqrt{n}}{\nu_{\min}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\\
\leq&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left|\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right|\left|E\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|+\frac{8\sqrt{n}}{\nu_{\min}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\\
\leq&n\left(\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{8}{\nu_{\min}}\right)\cdot\frac{4\sqrt{s}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{8\sqrt{n}}{\nu_{\min}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\\
\leq&Cn\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}+8\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}.\end{split}

(111)

Combine (
109
) and (
111
) together, we know that there exists a large constant
C
, for
1\leq x\lesssim n
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq\sqrt{n}\frac{C}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\sqrt{x}+Cn\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\nu_{\min}^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right)\leq e^{-x}.

Since
\nu_{\min}\geq\frac{a^{2}}{b^{2}}\frac{\bar{\nu}}{p}
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq C\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\sqrt{x}+Cn\sqrt{s}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right)\leq e^{-x}.

Therefore,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\|_{\infty}\geq C\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\sqrt{x}+Cn\sqrt{s}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq p,\left|\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq C\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\sqrt{x}+Cn\sqrt{s}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right)\\
\leq&pe^{-x}.\end{split}

Set
x=\left(C^{\prime}+1\right)\log p
, where
C^{\prime}
is an absolute constant, of course
x\lesssim n
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\|_{\infty}\geq C\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}+Cn\sqrt{s}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right)\leq e^{-C^{\prime}\log p}.

(112)

Choose
t=C\sqrt{n\log p\cdot\sigma^{2}}
in (
105
) and notice that
n\geq Cs\log p
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\epsilon\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\cdot\sigma^{2}}\right)\leq 2e^{-C^{\prime}\log p}.

Since
\sqrt{x}+\sqrt{y}\leq\sqrt{2}\sqrt{x+y}
, we know that

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\left(\sigma^{2}+\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+Cn^{2}s\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{3-2\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\|_{\infty}\geq C\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}+Cn\sqrt{s}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\epsilon\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\cdot\sigma^{2}}\right)\\
\leq&3e^{-C^{\prime}\log p}=3p^{-C^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(113)

Note that
\nu_{\min}\gtrsim\frac{\bar{\nu}}{p}\geq C\log(np)
, by (
71
) and the previous inequality, immediately we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{A}}_{W}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W}^{\top}y\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\left(\sigma^{2}+\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+Cn^{2}s\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{3-2\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\left(\sigma^{2}+\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+Cn^{2}s\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{3-2\epsilon}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq n,1\leq j\leq p,W_{ij}\neq W^{\prime}_{ij}\right)\\
\leq&3p^{-C^{\prime}}+2npe^{-c\nu_{\min}}\leq 3p^{-C^{\prime}}+2np\exp\left(-(C^{\prime}+1)\log(2np)\right)\leq 4p^{-C^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(114)

Proof of Lemma
B.6
.
By Taylor’s expansion, for
x>-1
,

\log(x+1)=x-\frac{x^{2}}{2}+\frac{x^{3}}{3(1+\xi)^{3}}

for some number
\xi
between 0 and
x
.
Take
x=\frac{t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}
, where
t\geq 0,z_{i}=\frac{N_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{2(1+\alpha_{i})}>0
,
\nu_{ij}=\nu_{i}X_{ij}
, we have

\log(t+z_{i})=\log\nu_{ij}+\frac{t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}+\frac{\left(t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{3}}{3\left(1+\xi_{t}\right)^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}},

where
\xi_{t}
is a real number between 0 and
\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}
.
Denote
f(t)=\log(t+z_{i})-\log\nu_{ij}-\frac{t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\left(t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}
. For
\nu_{ij}-\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}\leq t\leq\nu_{ij}+\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}
,
\exists C(\epsilon)>0
, if
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\epsilon)
,

\left|\frac{(t-\nu_{ij})^{3}}{3(1+\xi_{t})^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}}\right|\leq\frac{\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}+3\epsilon}}{2\nu_{ij}^{3}}.

Since
\nu_{ij}\geq\zeta_{i}^{1+\delta}
, set
\epsilon=\frac{\delta}{24+12\delta}<\frac{\delta}{12+6\delta}
, we have

\left|\frac{(t-\nu_{ij})^{3}}{3(1+\xi_{t})^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}}\right|\leq\frac{\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}+3\frac{\delta}{12+6\delta}}}{2\nu_{ij}^{3}}=\frac{\zeta_{i}\cdot\zeta_{i}^{\frac{1+\delta}{2+\delta}}}{2\nu_{ij}^{\frac{3}{2}-3\frac{\delta}{12+6\delta}}}\leq\frac{\zeta_{i}\cdot\nu_{ij}^{\frac{1}{2+\delta}}}{2\nu_{ij}^{\frac{3}{2}-3\frac{\delta}{12+6\delta}}}=\frac{\zeta_{i}}{2\nu_{ij}}.

(115)

Denote
r_{ij}=\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\delta}{24+12\delta}}
, then
r_{ij}\leq\left(\nu_{ij}^{\frac{2+\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)^{\frac{12+7\delta}{24+12\delta}}=\nu_{ij}^{\frac{12+7\delta}{12+12\delta}}<\nu_{ij}
. If
\nu_{i}\geq C(\delta)\geq C
, then
N_{i}\sim\nu_{i}
implies
EN_{i}^{k}\leq 2\nu_{i}^{k}
for
k=1,2,3
, and

Ez_{i}^{3}=E\left(\frac{N_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}\right)^{3}\leq 2\left(\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{2\left(\alpha_{i}+1\right)}\right)^{3}=2\zeta_{i}^{3}.

Also note that
\nu_{ij}\geq\zeta_{i}^{1+\delta}
,

E\frac{z_{i}^{3}}{3\left(1+\xi_{t}\right)^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}}1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}<\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq E\frac{z_{i}^{3}}{3\left(1+\xi_{t}\right)^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{i}^{3}}{2\nu_{ij}^{3}}\leq\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.

Combine (
115
) and the previous inequality together,

\begin{split}&E\left|f(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|=E\left|\frac{\left(t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{3}}{3\left(1+\xi_{t}\right)^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}}1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\\
\leq&4\left(E\left|\frac{(t-\nu_{ij})^{3}}{3(1+\xi_{t})^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}}1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}<\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|+E\frac{z_{i}^{3}}{3\left(1+\xi_{t}\right)^{3}\nu_{ij}^{3}}1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}<\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right)\\
\leq&4\left(\frac{\zeta_{i}}{2\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right)=6\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(116)

The first inequality holds since
\left|(t+z_{i}-\nu_{ij})^{3}\right|\leq 4\left(|t-\nu_{ij}|^{3}+z_{i}^{3}\right).

For
t\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij},\nu_{ij}\geq 1
, we have

\begin{split}\left|f(t)\right|\leq&\max\{\log\left(t+z_{i}\right),\log\nu_{ij}\}+\left|\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right|+\left|\frac{\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right|\\
\leq&\log(\nu_{ij}+z_{i})+\left|\frac{t-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\right|+\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+2\frac{(t-\nu_{ij})^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}+2\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\\
\leq&\left(\log(\nu_{ij})+\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right)+1+\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+1+\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\\
\leq&\log\nu_{ij}+2+\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}.\end{split}

The third inequality holds since
\log(u+v)\leq\log(u)+\frac{v}{u}
for
u,v>0
, and
|t-\nu_{ij}|\leq\nu_{ij}
for
0\leq t\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}
.
By Lemma
C.5
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-t\right)\leq 3\exp\left(-ct^{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\nu_{i}\cdot\alpha_{i}X_{ij}},\frac{\alpha_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{i}^{2}\cdot\alpha_{i}X_{ij}}\right\}\right)\leq 3\exp\left(-ct^{2}\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\alpha_{i}+\nu_{i}}\right).

Set
t=r_{ij}
and note that

r_{ij}^{2}\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\alpha_{i}+\nu_{i}}\geq\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{2(1+\epsilon)}\frac{1}{4\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}}=\frac{\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{2\epsilon}}{4},

where
\epsilon=\frac{\delta}{24+12\delta}
. Also note that
\zeta_{i}\geq\frac{1}{2}
, we have

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right)&\leq 3\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{2\epsilon}\right)\leq 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

(117)

Therefore
\exists C(\delta)
, if
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)
,

\begin{split}&\left|Ef(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\}}\right|\\
\leq&(\log\nu_{ij}+2){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right)+\frac{2}{\nu_{ij}}Ez_{i}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\}}+\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}Ez_{i}^{2}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\}}\\
\leq&(\log\nu_{ij}+2)3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+\frac{2}{\nu_{ij}}(Ez_{i}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}(E1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(Ez_{i}^{4})^{\frac{1}{2}}(E1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&(\log\nu_{ij}+2)3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+\frac{2}{\nu_{ij}}\cdot 2\zeta_{i}\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}2\zeta_{i}^{2}\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\\
\leq&(\log\nu_{ij}+2)3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+6\left(2\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\leq\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(118)

In the second inequality, we used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality; the third inequality comes from
Ez_{i}^{2}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{2},Ez_{i}^{4}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{4}
for
z_{i}\geq C(\delta)\geq C
.

For
t\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij},\nu_{ij}\geq 1
,

\begin{split}\left|f(t)\right|\leq&\left|\log\left(t+z_{i}\right)-\log\nu_{ij}\right|+\left|\frac{\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}-\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right|\\
=&\left|\log\left(t+z_{i}\right)-\log\nu_{ij}\right|+\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\left|\frac{t+z_{i}}{2\nu_{ij}}-\frac{3}{2}\right|\\
\leq&\log\left(t+z_{i}\right)+\frac{3\left(t+z_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\leq t+z_{i}+\frac{3\left(t+z_{i}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}.\end{split}

Therefore, for
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{3}\left(\delta\right)
, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}&\left|Ef(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\\
\leq&E(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i})1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}+3E\frac{(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i})^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\\
\leq&EW_{ij}^{\prime}\cdot 1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}+6E\frac{W_{ij}^{\prime 2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}+6E\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}+Ez_{i}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}.\end{split}

(119)

The last inequality is due to Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

Notice that
\exists
constant
C>0
such that
Eq_{ij}^{k}\leq CX_{ij}^{k}
and
E\left(W_{ij}^{k}|q_{ij}\right)\leq C\left(\nu_{i}q_{ij}\right)^{k}
for
1\leq k\leq 4
, we have

EW_{ij}^{k}\leq C\nu_{ij}^{k}

(120)

for
k=1,2,3,4
.
Similar to (
117
),

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right)&\leq 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

(121)

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}EW_{ij}1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\leq\left(EW_{ij}^{2}E1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\left(C\nu_{ij}^{2}\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\nu_{ij}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

(122)

\begin{split}EW_{ij}^{2}1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\leq\left(EW_{ij}^{4}E1_{\left\{W_{ij}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\left(C\nu_{ij}^{4}\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

(123)

In addition, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality tells us

\begin{split}E\left|W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right|1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq&\left(E\left(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)^{2}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

(124)

Since

\begin{split}E\left(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)^{2}=&E\left(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)^{2}1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}\leq 2E\left[(W_{ij}^{2}+W_{ij}^{\prime 2})1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}\right]\\
\leq&2E\left[(W_{ij}^{2}+\nu_{ij}^{2})1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}\right]\leq 2EW_{ij}^{2}+2\nu_{ij}^{2}\leq C\nu_{ij}^{2}+2\nu_{ij}^{2},\end{split}

By (
121
) and (
124
), we have

\begin{split}E\left|W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right|1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq&\left[C\nu_{ij}^{2}\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\nu_{ij}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

Combine (
122
) and the previous inequality together,

EW_{ij}^{\prime}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq EW_{ij}1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}+E\left|W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right|1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq C\nu_{ij}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).

(125)

Similarly,

\begin{split}&E\left|W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2}\right|1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq\left(E\left(W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2}\right)^{2}E1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\left[\left(2EW_{ij}^{4}+2\nu_{ij}^{4}\right)\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\left[\left(C\nu_{ij}^{4}+2\nu_{ij}^{4}\right)\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

(126)

(
123
) and (
126
) imply that

\begin{split}EW_{ij}^{\prime 2}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq E\left|W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2}\right|1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}+EW_{ij}^{2}1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\leq C\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

(127)

Also, by (
121
) and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and notice that
\nu_{ij}^{2}\geq\left(\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{\alpha+1}\right)^{2}=\zeta_{i}^{2}
,

\begin{split}\left(Ez_{i}^{2}1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\right)^{2}\leq Ez_{i}^{4}E1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{4}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right)\leq 6\nu_{ij}^{4}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right),\end{split}

(128)

\begin{split}\left(Ez_{i}1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\right)^{2}\leq Ez_{i}^{2}E1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right\}}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{2}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\right)\leq 6\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right).\end{split}

(129)

Combine (
119
), (
125
), (
127
), (
128
) and (
129
) together, we know that for
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)=C(\epsilon)
,

\begin{split}&\left|Ef(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\\
\leq&C\nu_{ij}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+C\nu_{ij}\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{\epsilon}\right)\\
\leq&\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(130)

Combine (
116
), (
118
) and (
130
), we know that
\exists C(\delta)>0
such that for all
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)
,

\left|Ef(W_{ij}^{\prime})\right|\leq E\left|f(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|+\left|Ef(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\}}\right|+\left|Ef(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\leq 8\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.

(131)

Next, we would like to bound
\left|E\left(\frac{W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\right|
. Actually,

\begin{split}&\left|E\left(\frac{W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\right|\\
=&\left|E\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}-E\frac{(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}-E\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}-E\frac{z_{i}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right|\\
\leq&\left|\frac{\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}}{\nu_{i}X_{ij}}-\frac{\nu_{i}X_{ij}+\nu_{i}^{2}\frac{X_{ij}(1-X_{ij})}{\alpha_{i}+1}}{2\nu_{i}^{2}X_{ij}^{2}}\right|+E\frac{z_{i}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}+E\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\\
=&\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}+\frac{Ez_{i}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}+\frac{Ez_{i}^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}.\end{split}

Note that
E\zeta_{i}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})=\zeta_{i}E(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})=0
and
Ez_{i}^{2}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{2}
if
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)\geq C
, we have

\begin{split}&\left|E\left(\frac{W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}+\frac{E(z_{i}-\zeta_{i})(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}+\frac{Ez_{i}^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}+\frac{1}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(E\left(\frac{N_{i}-\nu_{i}}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(E(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})^{2}\right)^{1/2}+\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}+\frac{1}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(\frac{\nu_{i}}{4(\alpha_{i}+1)^{2}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\nu_{i}X_{ij}+\nu_{i}^{2}\frac{X_{ij}(1-X_{ij})}{\alpha_{i}+1}\right)^{1/2}+\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}.\end{split}

Since
\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}=\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}\leq\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{i}}\asymp\frac{\zeta_{i}}{p\nu_{ij}}
,
\frac{\nu_{i}}{4(\alpha_{i}+1)^{2}}\leq\frac{\zeta_{i}}{2(\alpha_{i}+1)}\leq\zeta_{i}
and
\nu_{i}X_{ij}+\nu_{i}^{2}\frac{X_{ij}(1-X_{ij})}{\alpha_{i}+1}\leq\nu_{i}X_{ij}+\nu_{i}^{2}\frac{X_{ij}}{\alpha_{i}+1}=2\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}
, we have

\begin{split}&\left|E\left(\frac{W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\right|\leq C\frac{\zeta_{i}}{p\nu_{ij}}+\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(\zeta_{i}\right)^{1/2}\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{1/2}+\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\leq\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(132)

The last inequality holds since
\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\zeta_{i}}\geq\frac{\bar{\nu}}{p\zeta_{\max}}\geq C(\delta)\geq C
and
\nu_{ij}\geq Cp\zeta_{i}\geq C
.

Denote
\mathcal{T}_{ij}=\left[\frac{X_{ij}}{10},10X_{ij}\right]
, by Lemma
C.2
and
C.4
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q_{ij}\notin\mathcal{T}_{ij}\right)+E\left[{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}|q_{ij}\right)1_{\left\{q_{ij}\in\mathcal{T}_{ij}\right\}}\right]\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q_{ij}<\frac{1}{10}X_{ij}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(q_{ij}>10X_{ij}\right)+E\left[\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}<\frac{1}{10}\nu_{i}q_{ij}|q_{ij}\right)\right)1_{\left\{q_{ij}\in\mathcal{T}_{ij}\right\}}\right]\\
&+E\left[\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}>10\nu_{i}q_{ij}|q_{ij}\right)\right)1_{\left\{q_{ij}\in\mathcal{T}_{ij}\right\}}\right]\\
\leq&4\exp\left(-cp\alpha_{i}X_{ij}^{2}\right)+2E\exp\left(-c\nu_{i}q_{ij}\right)1_{\left\{q_{ij}\in\mathcal{T}_{ij}\right\}}\\
\leq&4\exp\left(-c\frac{\alpha_{i}}{p}\right)+2\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}\right).\end{split}

(133)

Since
\nu_{ij}\geq\zeta_{i}^{1+\delta}\gtrsim\left(\frac{\nu_{i}}{\alpha_{i}}\right)^{1+\delta}\gtrsim\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{1+\delta}\left(\frac{p}{\alpha_{i}}\right)^{1+\delta}
, we have

\frac{\alpha_{i}}{p}\gtrsim\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}.

(134)

Therefore,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\leq 6\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right),

which means

\begin{split}E|W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}|=&E|W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}|1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}=E|W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}|1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}\\
\leq&\left(E(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})^{2}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\left(2\left(EW_{ij}^{2}+\nu_{ij}^{2}\right)6\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&C\nu_{ij}\exp\left(-c(\nu_{ij})^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right).\end{split}

(135)

In the first and second inequality, we used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality; the third inequality comes from (
120
).

Similarly,

\begin{split}E|W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2}|=&E|W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2}|1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}=E|W_{ij}^{2}-\nu_{ij}^{2}|1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}\\
\leq&\left(E(W_{ij}^{2}-\nu_{ij}^{2})^{2}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\left(2\left(EW_{ij}^{4}+\nu_{ij}^{4}\right)6\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&C\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c(\nu_{ij})^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right).\end{split}

(136)

\begin{split}E(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime})^{2}=&E(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime})^{2}1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}=E(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})^{2}1_{\{W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\}}\\
\leq&\left(E(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})^{4}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\left(8\left(EW_{ij}^{4}+\nu_{ij}^{4}\right)6\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&C\nu_{ij}^{2}\exp\left(-c(\nu_{ij})^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right).\end{split}

(137)

Combine (
132
), (
136
) and (
137
) together,

\begin{split}&\left|E\left(\frac{W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\left|E\left(\frac{W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(W_{ij}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\right|+2\left|E\frac{W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}}{\nu_{ij}}\right|\\
&+\left|E\frac{W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right|+\left|E\frac{z_{i}(W_{ij}^{\prime}-W_{ij})}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right|\\
\leq&\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+C\exp\left(-c(\nu_{ij})^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)+\frac{\left(Ez_{i}^{2}E\left(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\\
\leq&\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+C\exp\left(-c(\nu_{ij})^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)+\frac{C\zeta_{i}\nu_{ij}\exp\left(-c(\nu_{ij})^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\leq 2\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(138)

In the second inequality we also used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality; the third inequality comes from (
137
) and
Ez_{i}^{2}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{2}
if
\nu_{i}\geq C
.

(
131
) and (
138
) together imply that

\begin{split}\left|E\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}\right)-\log\nu_{ij}\right|\leq&\left|E\left(\frac{W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right)\right|+\left|Ef(W_{ij}^{\prime})\right|\leq 10\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

Proof of Lemma
B.7
.

\log^{2}(\nu_{ij}+x)=\log^{2}\nu_{ij}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}x+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}x^{2}+\frac{2\log(\nu_{ij}+\xi)-3}{3(\nu_{ij}+\xi)^{3}}x^{3},

where
\xi
is a real number between 0 and
x
.

Let
x=t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}
, we have

\begin{split}\log^{2}\left(t+z_{i}\right)=&\log^{2}\nu_{ij}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{2}\\
&+\frac{2\log(\nu_{ij}+\xi_{t})-3}{3(\nu_{ij}+\xi_{t})^{3}}\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{3},\end{split}

where
\xi_{t}
is a real number between 0 and
t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}
.

Let
g(t)=\log^{2}(t+z_{i})-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}-2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})-\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})^{2}
, then for any
\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<t<\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}
(where
r_{ij}=\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\delta}{24+12\delta}}
defined in the proof of Lemma
B.6
),
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)
, we have

\begin{split}|g(t)|\leq&\frac{2\log(\nu_{ij}+\xi_{t})}{3(\nu_{ij}+\xi_{t})^{3}}\left|t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right|^{3}\leq\frac{2\log\left(\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right)}{3\left(\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right)^{3}}\left(r_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{3}\leq\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{3}}\cdot 4\left(r_{ij}^{3}+z_{i}^{3}\right)\\
=&4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{3}}\left(\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\delta}{8+4\delta}}+4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{3}}z_{i}^{3}\leq 4\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{\log(\nu_{ij})\nu_{ij}^{\frac{4+3\delta}{(8+4\delta)(\delta+1)}}}{\nu_{ij}^{\frac{4+\delta}{8+4\delta}}}+4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{3}}z_{i}^{3}\\
=&4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{\frac{\delta}{4(1+\delta)}}}\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{3}}z_{i}^{3}.\end{split}

The second inequality holds since
\xi_{t}\geq t-\nu_{ij}\geq-r_{ij}
and
\frac{\log t}{t^{3}}
is a decreasing function of
t\geq e^{\frac{1}{3}}
; the third inequality comes from
(u+v)^{3}\leq 4(u^{3}+v^{3})
for
u,v\geq 0
and
r_{ij}\leq\nu_{ij}^{1-\frac{5\delta}{12+12\delta}}
; the last inequality is due to
\nu_{ij}\geq\zeta_{i}^{1+\delta}
.

Since
\nu_{ij}\gtrsim\zeta_{i}^{1+\delta}
and
Ez_{i}^{3}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{3}
for
\nu_{i}\geq C
, we know that if
\nu_{i}>C
,

\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}<\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\leq 4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{\frac{\delta}{4(1+\delta)}}}\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+8\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{3}}\zeta_{i}^{3}\leq 4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{\frac{\delta}{4(1+\delta)}}}\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+8\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.

Therefore
\exists C_{1}(\delta)>0
, for all
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{1}(\delta)
,

\displaystyle\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}<\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\leq 2\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.

(139)

For
t\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}
,
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{2}(\delta)
, we have

\begin{split}\left|g(t)\right|\leq&\max\{\log^{2}\left(t+z_{i}\right),\log^{2}\nu_{ij}\}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\left|\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right|+\left|\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\right|\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\log^{2}(\nu_{ij}+z_{i})+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\left(\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right)+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\left(\log\nu_{ij}+\frac{z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right)^{2}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}z_{i}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\log^{2}\nu_{ij}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\log\nu_{ij}+6\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}z_{i}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}z_{i}^{2}.\end{split}

Denote
\epsilon=\frac{\delta}{24+12\delta}
, similarly to (
118
), for
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{2}(\delta)
,

\begin{split}&\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right\}}\right|\\
=&\left|E\left(\log^{2}\nu_{ij}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\log\nu_{ij}\right)1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right\}}\right|+6\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}(Ez_{i}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(E1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right\}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(Ez_{i}^{4})^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(E1_{\left\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right\}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\left(\log^{2}\nu_{ij}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\log\nu_{ij}\right){\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right)+C\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\zeta_{i}\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+C\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\zeta_{i}^{2}\left({\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\left(\log^{2}\nu_{ij}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\log\nu_{ij}\right)\cdot 3\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)+C\left(\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\zeta_{i}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\zeta_{i}^{2}\right)\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}^{2\epsilon}\right)\\
\leq&C\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(140)

\forall t>\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}
and
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{3}(\delta)
, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}g(t)\leq&\left|\log^{2}\left(t+z_{i}\right)-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}\right|+\left|2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)^{2}\right|\\
\leq&\log^{2}\left(t+z_{i}\right)+\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\left|2\log\nu_{ij}+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\log^{2}\left(t+z_{i}\right)+\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\left(2\log\nu_{ij}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\left(t-\nu_{ij}+z_{i}\right)\right)\\
\leq&\left(\log t+\frac{z_{i}}{t}\right)^{2}+2(\log\nu_{ij})\frac{t+z_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(t+z_{i})^{2}\\
\leq&2\log^{2}t+2\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{t^{2}}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}t+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}z_{i}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}t^{2}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}z_{i}^{2}\\
\leq&t+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}t+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}t^{2}+2\frac{z_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}z_{i}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}z_{i}^{2}.\end{split}

Use the same method in Lemma
B.6
, we have

\begin{split}\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\leq C\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(141)

Combine (
139
), (
140
) and the previous inequality together, for
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)=\max\{C_{1}(\delta),C_{2}(\delta),C_{3}(\delta)\}
, we have

\begin{split}\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})\right|=&\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}<W_{ij}^{\prime}<\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|+\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\nu_{ij}-r_{ij}\}}\right|+\left|Eg(W_{ij}^{\prime})1_{\{W_{ij}^{\prime}\geq\nu_{ij}+r_{ij}\}}\right|\\
\leq&C\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(142)

In addition, for
\nu_{ij}\geq C(\delta)\geq C
, we have

\begin{split}&\left|E\left[2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})^{2}\right]\right|\\
\leq&\left|2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}Ez_{i}+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}E\left(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}\right|+\left|\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}Ez_{i}^{2}\right|+\left|2\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}E(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})z_{i}\right|\\
\leq&\left|2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{2\left(\alpha_{i}+1\right)}+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(\nu_{i}X_{ij}+\nu_{i}^{2}\frac{X_{ij}(1-X_{ij})}{\alpha_{i}+1}\right)\right|+\log(\nu_{ij})\cdot 2\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\\
&+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(E\left(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(Ez_{i}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
=&\left|2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\zeta_{i}+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\left(2\zeta_{i}-\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\alpha_{i}+1}\right)\right|+2\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(\nu_{i}X_{ij}+\nu_{i}^{2}\frac{X_{ij}(1-X_{ij})}{\alpha_{i}+1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(Ez_{i}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&2\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\alpha_{i}+1}+2\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left(2\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{2}\zeta_{i}\\
\leq&2\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{\nu_{i}(\alpha_{i}+1)}+2\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}+4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}\\
\leq&2\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+2\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{i}}+2\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}+4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}\zeta_{i}\leq 10\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

In the second inequality, we used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and
Ez_{i}^{2}\leq 2\zeta_{i}^{2}
for
\nu_{i}\geq C
; the fourth inequality and the fifth inequality come from
\nu_{ij}\geq\zeta_{i}
.
By (
135
), (
136
) and (
137
) and the previous inequality, we have

\begin{split}&\left|E\left[2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}(W_{ij}^{\prime}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(W_{ij}^{\prime}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})^{2}\right]\right|\\
\leq&\left|E\left[2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})^{2}\right]\right|+\frac{4\log(\nu_{ij})-2}{\nu_{ij}}E\left|W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right|\\
&+2\frac{\log(\nu_{ij})-1}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left|E\left[(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime})z_{i}\right]\right|+\frac{\log(\nu_{ij})-1}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left|E(W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2})\right|\\
\leq&\left|E\left[2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})+\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}+z_{i})^{2}\right]\right|\\
&+4\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}E\left|W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime}\right|+2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left[E(W_{ij}-W_{ij}^{\prime})^{2}Ez_{i}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left|E\left(W_{ij}^{2}-W_{ij}^{\prime 2}\right)\right|\\
\leq&10\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+C\log(\nu_{ij})\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)+C\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\\
&+C\log(\nu_{ij})\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\right)^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\\
\leq&C\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}.\end{split}

(143)

Combine (
142
) and (
143
) together, we have

\left|E\log^{2}(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i})-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}\right|\leq C\log(\nu_{ij})\frac{\zeta}{\nu_{ij}}.

(144)

Proof of Lemma
B.8
.
For convenience, denote
D_{ij}=\log\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}+z_{i}\right)-E\log\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}+z_{i}\right)
, by Lemma
B.6
,
\left|E\log\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}+z_{i}\right)-\log\nu_{ij}\right|\leq 10\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}
. By lemma
C.5
, for
t\geq 1
, note that
\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}<1
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sqrt{\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\zeta_{i}}}D_{ij}\right|>t\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}\right)>E\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}\right)<E\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+z_{i}\right)-\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}+z_{i}\right)>\log\nu_{ij}-10\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}+\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\log\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}+z_{i}\right)<\log\nu_{ij}+10\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}-\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}>\nu_{ij}e^{\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t-10\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}-z_{i}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}<\nu_{ij}e^{-\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t+10\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}-z_{i}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}>\nu_{ij}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-z_{i}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}<\nu_{ij}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-z_{i}\right).\end{split}

(145)

We first consider
{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}>\nu_{ij}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-z_{i}\right)
.
\forall t\geq 10
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}>\nu_{ij}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-z_{i}\right)\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}>\nu_{ij}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(z_{i}\geq\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\right).\end{split}

For
t\geq 10
,
\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\geq\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{\alpha_{i}+1}=2Ez_{i}
, by Lemma
C.1
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(z_{i}\geq\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\right)=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(z_{i}-Ez_{i}\geq\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}-Ez_{i}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(N_{i}-\nu_{i}\geq 2\left(\alpha_{i}+1\right)\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{200(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\right)\\
\leq&\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\frac{1}{100}\left(\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1\right)t^{2}\right)^{2}}{\nu_{i}}\psi_{Benn}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{100}\left(\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1\right)t^{2}}{\nu_{i}}\right)\right).\end{split}

Since for all
t\geq 10
,
\frac{\frac{1}{100}\left(\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1\right)t^{2}}{\nu_{i}}\geq 1
, which means
\psi_{Benn}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{100}\left(\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1\right)t^{2}}{\nu_{i}}\right)\geq\frac{c}{\frac{1}{100}\left(\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1\right)t^{2}/\nu_{i}}
. Therefore,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(z_{i}\geq\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\right)\leq e^{-c(\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1)t^{2}}.\end{split}

(146)

Since
e^{x}\geq\frac{x^{2}}{2}
for all
x>0
, for
\nu_{ij}
large eough, we have

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}\nu_{ij}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-1\right)-\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\geq&\frac{1}{2}\nu_{ij}\frac{\left(\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t\right)^{2}}{2}-\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\geq\frac{1}{16}\zeta_{i}t^{2}-\frac{1}{50}\zeta_{i}t^{2}\geq 0.\end{split}

By Lemma
C.5
, for
\nu_{ij}
large enough and
t\geq 1
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}>\nu_{ij}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}-\nu_{ij}>\nu_{ij}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-1\right)-\frac{\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1}{100(\alpha_{i}+1)}t^{2}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}-\nu_{ij}>\frac{1}{2}\nu_{ij}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-1\right)\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left(-c\left(\frac{1}{2}\nu_{ij}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-1\right)\right)^{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\nu_{i}\cdot\alpha_{i}X_{ij}},\frac{\alpha_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{i}^{2}\cdot\alpha_{i}X_{ij}}\right\}\right)\\
=&2\exp\left(-c\left(\frac{1}{2}\nu_{ij}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-1\right)\right)^{2}\min\left\{\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}},\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\nu_{i}}\right\}\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left(-c\left(\frac{1}{2}\nu_{ij}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-1\right)\right)^{2}\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{1}{\zeta_{i}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-c\left(\frac{1}{2}\nu_{ij}\cdot\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t\right)^{2}\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{1}{\zeta_{i}}\right)\\
\leq&2\exp(-ct^{2}).\end{split}

By (
146
) and the previous inequality, for
t\geq 1
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}>\nu_{ij}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-z_{i}\right)\leq\exp\left(-c(\nu_{i}+\alpha_{i}+1)t^{2}\right)+2\exp\left(-ct^{2}\right).

(147)

Moreover, when
t>10\sqrt{\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\zeta_{i}}}
, we have
\nu_{ij}e^{-5}-z_{i}\leq\frac{1}{100}\nu_{ij}
, thus

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W^{\prime}_{ij}<\nu_{ij}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-z_{i}\right)=0.

When
t\leq 10\sqrt{\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\zeta_{i}}}
, note that
\frac{1-e^{-x}}{x}
is decreasing, by Lemma
C.5
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}<\nu_{ij}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}-z_{i}\right)\leq{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}<\nu_{ij}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}<-\nu_{ij}\left(1-e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}\right)\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\left(1-e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}\right)\right)^{2}\min\left\{\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\nu_{i}\cdot\alpha_{i}X_{ij}},\frac{\alpha_{i}^{2}}{\nu_{i}^{2}\cdot\alpha_{i}X_{ij}}\right\}\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left(-c\left(\nu_{ij}\left(1-e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}\right)\right)^{2}\min\left\{\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}},\frac{1}{\nu_{ij}}\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}\right\}\right)\\
\leq&2\exp\left(-c\frac{\left(\nu_{ij}\left(1-e^{-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t}\right)\right)^{2}}{\nu_{ij}\zeta_{i}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-c\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\zeta_{i}}\frac{\left(1-e^{-5}\right)^{2}}{5^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ij}}}t\right)^{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-ct^{2}\right).\end{split}

By (
147
) and the previous inequality, and use the same method in Lemma
B.3
, we can get the result.

Proof of Lemma
B.9
.

\begin{split}&\left\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}=\left\|{P}{A}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-{P}{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}(V\beta^{*}+\varepsilon)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\left\|{P}({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}+\|\bar{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

For
\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon
, we have

\begin{split}\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}=&\|{P}{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}\leq\|{P}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}+\|{P}E{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}\\
\leq&2\|\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}+\|{P}E{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}\end{split}

(148)

Note that for
1\leq i\leq p
,

\begin{split}\left[\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\varepsilon\right]_{i}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k},\end{split}

\forall t\geq 0
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t\Big|{W^{\prime}}_{lj},N_{l},1\leq l\leq n,1\leq j\leq p\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(B_{W^{\prime}}-EB_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}}}.

(149)

By Lemma
B.8
, there exists a constant
K>0
such that
\left\|\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}-E\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq K
. Thus
\left\|\left(\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}-E\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)^{2}\right\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 2K^{2}
. Consider the centering, we can get

\left\|\left(\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}-E\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)^{2}-E\left(\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}-E\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)^{2}\right\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 4K^{2}.

By Proposition 5.16 in
Vershynin 2010
,
\forall t\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}-E\left[\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\right]>t\right)\leq\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{t^{2}}{16nK^{4}},\frac{t}{4K^{2}}\right)\right],

where
c>0
is a constant.

Choose
t=n
and notice that
K
is a constant, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\geq n+E\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\right)\leq e^{-cn}.

Also, apply Lemma
B.6
and Lemma
B.7
, and also recall that
\nu_{ki}\geq\zeta_{i}^{1+\delta}
, for
\nu_{\min}\geq C(\delta)
,

\begin{split}E\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}=&E\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}-\left(E\left(B_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right)^{2}\leq\log^{2}\nu_{ki}+C\log(\nu_{ki})\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ki}}-\left(\log\nu_{ki}-C\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ki}}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&C\frac{\log\nu_{ki}}{\nu_{ki}^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}}+2C\frac{\log\nu_{ki}}{\nu_{ki}^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}}\leq C.\end{split}

Thus

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\geq Cn\right)\leq e^{-cn}.

By (
149
) and the previous inequality,
\forall t\geq 0
,
\exists
a constant
c>0
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}<Cn\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}\geq Cn\right)\\
\leq&2e^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}+e^{-cn}.\end{split}

Therefore

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\varepsilon\right\|_{\infty}>t\right)\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq p,\left|\left[\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\varepsilon\right]_{i}\right|>t\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq p,\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t\right)\\
\leq&p\left(2e^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}+e^{-cn}\right).\end{split}

(150)

Notice that

\begin{split}\left({P}E{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\right)_{i}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}{P}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\right)\varepsilon_{k},\end{split}

\forall t\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\right)\varepsilon_{k}>t\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\right)^{2}}}.

Since

\begin{split}\left|E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{ki})-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}(W^{\prime}_{kj})\right|\leq\left|\log\nu_{ki}-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\log\nu_{kj}\right|+C\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{ki}}+\frac{1}{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}C\frac{\zeta_{i}}{\nu_{kj}}\leq C,\end{split}

we know that

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left(\left|{P}E{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\prime\top}\varepsilon\right)_{i}\right|>t\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}.

Hence,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|{P}E{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\prime\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}>t\right)={\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq p,\left|\left({P}E{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\right)_{i}\right|>t\right)\leq 2pe^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}.\end{split}

(151)

Combine (
148
), (
150
) and (
151
) together,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\right\|_{\infty}>t\right)\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}>\frac{t}{4}\right)+E\left(\|{P}E{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}>\frac{t}{2}\right)\\
\leq&p\left(4e^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}+e^{-cn}\right).\end{split}

(152)

For the second part,

\begin{split}\left\|{P}{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}\leq&\left\|{P}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{P}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}\\
\leq&2\left\|\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{P}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

Denote
D_{ki}=\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)
,
S=\text{supp}(\beta^{*})=\{1\leq j\leq p:\beta^{*}_{j}\neq 0\}
. By Lemma
B.8
, for all
1\leq i\leq p
,

\begin{split}\left[\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}=&\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\right)\left(\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\\
=&\sum_{j\in S}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\right)\left(\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\\
=&\sum_{j\in S}\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}D_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}+\sum_{j\in S}\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}(D_{kj}-\log\nu_{kj})\beta_{j}^{*}.\end{split}

(153)

\forall j\in S
,
p\geq 1
, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}\frac{1}{p}\left(E\left|XY\right|^{p}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\frac{1}{p}\left(E\left|X\right|^{2p}E\left|Y\right|^{2p}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}=2\frac{1}{\sqrt{2p}}\left(E\left|X\right|^{2p}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2p}}\left(E\left|Y\right|^{2p}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}.\end{split}

Thus

\begin{split}&\|D_{ki}D_{kj}-ED_{ki}D_{kj}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 2\|D_{ki}D_{kj}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq 4\|D_{ki}\|_{\psi_{2}}\|D_{kj}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq C\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}.\end{split}

By Proposition 5.16 in
Vershynin 2010
,
\forall t\geq 0
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}D_{kj}-\sum_{k=1}^{n}ED_{ki}D_{kj}\right|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left[-c\min\left(\frac{\nu_{\min}^{2}t^{2}}{n\zeta_{\max}^{2}},\frac{\nu_{\min}t}{\zeta_{\max}}\right)\right].\end{split}

(154)

By Lemma
B.6
and Lemma
B.7
, if
\nu_{ki}\geq\max\{C(\delta),\zeta_{k}^{1+\delta}\}
,

\begin{split}ED_{ki}^{2}=&E\phi^{2}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)\right)^{2}\leq\log^{2}(\nu_{ki})+C\log(\nu_{ki})\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{ki}}-\left(\log(\nu_{ki})-10\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{ki}}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&C\log(\nu_{ki})\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{ki}}+20\log(\nu_{ki})\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{ki}}\leq C\log(\nu_{ki})\frac{1}{\nu_{ki}^{\frac{\delta}{1+\delta}}}\leq C.\end{split}

\begin{split}\left|ED_{ki}D_{kj}\right|\leq\left(ED_{ki}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(ED_{kj}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\left(\log\nu_{ki}\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{ki}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\log\nu_{kj}\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{kj}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}.\end{split}

Therefore, set
t=n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}
in (
154
), we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}D_{kj}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\right)\leq 2e^{-cn\log\nu_{\min}}.

(155)

In addition, by Proposition 5.10 in
Vershynin 2010
(note that
ED_{ki}=0
),
\forall t\geq 0
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\geq t\right)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-c\frac{\nu_{\min}t^{2}}{\zeta_{\max}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)^{2}}\right).

By Lemma
B.6
,

\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)^{2}\leq C\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{kj}}\right)^{2}\leq Cn\frac{\zeta_{\max}^{2}}{\nu_{\min}^{2}}.

Therefore,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\geq t\right)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-c\frac{\nu_{\min}^{3}t^{2}}{n\zeta_{\max}^{3}}\right).

Choose
t=n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}
, note that
\nu_{\min}\asymp\nu_{\max}\geq\zeta_{\max}^{1+\delta}
and
\nu_{ki}\geq C(\delta)
for all
k,i
, we have

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\geq n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\right)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-cn\log^{2}\nu_{\min}\right).\end{split}

By (
153
), (
155
) and the previous inequality, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left[\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{j\in S}\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}D_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{j\in S}\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}\left(D_{kj}-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{j\in S}\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}D_{kj}\right|\left|\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\sum_{j\in S}\left|\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{j\in S}\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}\left(D_{kj}-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\left|\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\sum_{j\in S}\left|\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\right)\\
\leq&\sum_{j\in S}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}D_{kj}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\right)\\
&+\sum_{j\in S}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}D_{ki}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\geq n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\right)\\
\leq&s(2+e)e^{-cn\log\nu_{\min}}.\end{split}

Therefore,
\exists
two large constant
C,C^{\prime}>0
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq p,\left|\left[\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&p\cdot s(2+e)e^{-cn\log\nu_{\min}}\leq p^{-C^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(156)

For all
1\leq i\leq p
,

\begin{split}\left[{P}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}=&\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kl}\right)\right)\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\\
=&\sum_{j\in S}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kl}\right)\right)\left(\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}.\end{split}

(157)

By Lemma
B.6
,

\begin{split}\left|E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kl}\right)\right|\leq\left|\log\nu_{ki}-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\log\nu_{kl}\right|+C\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{ki}}+\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}C\frac{\zeta_{k}}{\nu_{kl}}\leq C.\end{split}

Denote
E_{ki}=E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{ki}\right)-\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kl}\right)
, by Hoeffding-type inequality,
\forall j\in S,t\geq 0
,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right)\right|\geq t\right)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-c\frac{\nu_{\min}t^{2}}{\zeta_{\max}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}^{2}}\right)\leq e\cdot e^{-c\frac{\nu_{\min}t^{2}}{n\zeta_{\max}}},\end{split}

which is equivalent to

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)-\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(E\phi\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\geq t\right)\leq e\cdot e^{-c\frac{\nu_{\min}t^{2}}{n\zeta_{\max}}}.

(158)

Since
\left|E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right|\leq 10\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}
for all
1\leq k\leq n
, we know that

\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\leq 10n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}.

Choose
t=C\sqrt{n\log p}\frac{\sqrt{\zeta_{\max}}}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}
in (
158
), we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\right)\leq e^{-C^{\prime\prime}\log p}.

By (
157
) and the previous inequality,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\left[{P}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{j\in S}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\sum_{j\in S}\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\left|\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\sum_{j\in S}\left|\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\right)\\
\leq&\sum_{j\in S}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}E_{ki}\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\right|\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\right)\\
\leq&s\dot{e}^{-C^{\prime\prime}\log p}.\end{split}

Therefore, there exist two large constants
C,C^{\prime}>0
,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|{P}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq p,\left|\left[{P}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&ps\cdot p^{-C^{\prime\prime}}\leq p^{-C^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(159)

Combine (
152
), (
156
), (
159
) and (
117
), we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}\leq C\left[\sqrt{n\log p}\left\{\sigma+\left(\frac{pn}{\nu}\zeta_{\max}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right\}+n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right]\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\right\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\cdot\sigma^{2}}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|{P}\left(E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}\geq C\left\{\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+n\frac{\zeta_{\max}}{\nu_{\min}}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-E{B}_{W^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left({B}_{W^{\prime}}-V\right)\beta^{*}\right\|_{\infty}\geq Cn\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right)\\
\leq&4p^{-C^{\prime}}.\end{split}

Also notice that (
133
) holds, we have

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\bar{{A}}_{W}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}\leq C\left[\sqrt{n\log p}\left\{\sigma+\left(\frac{pn}{\nu}\zeta_{\max}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right\}+n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right]\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}\leq C\left[\sqrt{n\log p}\left\{\sigma+\left(\frac{pn}{\nu}\zeta_{\max}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right\}+n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right]\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists i,j,W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\\
\leq&4p^{-C^{\prime}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(4\exp\left(-c\frac{\alpha_{i}}{p}\right)+2\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}\right)\right).\end{split}

Noting that

\frac{\alpha_{i}}{p}\asymp\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{i}/\alpha_{i}}\geq\frac{\nu_{ij}}{\zeta_{i}}\geq\frac{\nu_{\min}}{\zeta_{\max}}\geq C\log(np)
, for any
1\leq i\leq n,1\leq j\leq p
,we have

4\exp\left(-c\frac{\alpha_{i}}{p}\right)\leq(np)^{-(C^{\prime}+1)}\leq\frac{1}{np}p^{-C^{\prime}},\quad 2\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}\right)\leq(np)^{-(C^{\prime}+1)}\leq\frac{1}{np}p^{-C^{\prime}}.

Therefore

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left\|\bar{{A}}_{W}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W}^{\top}y\right\|_{\infty}\leq C\left[\sqrt{n\log p}\left\{\sigma+\left(\frac{pn}{\nu}\zeta_{\max}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right\}+n\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}\zeta_{\max}\|\beta^{*}\|_{1}\right]\right)\leq 6p^{-C^{\prime}}.

Proof of Lemma
B.5
.
Let
V^{\prime}
be the random matrix with entries
v_{ij}^{\prime}=v_{ij}-v.

By Lemma 5 in
Achlioptas 2001
, for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},0<\epsilon<1
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V^{\prime}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\geq\epsilon\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right).

(160)

Since
V{P}=V^{\prime}{P}
, we know that for any fixed
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\geq\epsilon\|{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right).

(161)

For any
2s
-sparse
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
, denote
S_{0}
be the support set of
x
. By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}\|x\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|{P}x\|_{2}^{2}=\|x\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{p}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\right)^{2}=\|x\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{p}\left(\sum_{i\in S_{0}}x_{i}\right)^{2}\geq\|x\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{2s}{p}\|x\|_{2}^{2}=(1-\frac{2s}{p})\|x\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

If
\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\leq\epsilon\|{P}x\|_{2}^{2}
, then

\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\leq\left(1+\epsilon\right)\|{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\leq\left(1+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq\left(1+\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2},

and

\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\geq(1-\epsilon)\|{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\geq\left(1-\epsilon\right)\left(1-\frac{2s}{p}\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\geq\left(1-\frac{2s}{p}-\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}.

(
161
) and the previous two inequalities together imply

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\geq\left(\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right).

(162)

For any
S_{0}\subset\{1,2,\dots,p\},\left|S_{0}\right|=2s
, denote
\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}=\{x:x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\|x\|_{2}=1,\text{supp}(x)\subset S\}
, by
Vershynin 2010
, we can find an
\epsilon
-net of
\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}
, called
\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
, with
\left|\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}\right|\leq\left(1+\frac{2}{\epsilon}\right)^{2s}
. By Lemma
C.7
with
\tilde{{A}}=\left(V{P}\right)^{\top}\left(V{P}\right)
,

\sup_{x\in\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}}\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\leq(1-2\epsilon)^{-1}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}.

(163)

By (
162
) and the union bound,

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{\epsilon}{4}},\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\geq\left(\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\leq 2\left(1+\frac{8}{\epsilon}\right)^{2s}\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right),\end{split}

i.e.,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{\epsilon}{4}},\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|<\left(\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
\geq&1-2\left(1+\frac{8}{\epsilon}\right)^{2s}\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right).\end{split}

(164)

For
\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{3}
, if for any
x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{\epsilon}{4}}
,
\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|<\left(\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon
, then (
163
) shows us for any
x\in\mathcal{A}_{S}
,

\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{1-\frac{\epsilon}{2}}\left(1+\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\leq 1+\frac{2s}{p}+2\epsilon.

(165)

By the definition of
\epsilon
-net,
\exists y\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{\epsilon}{4}}
,
\|x-y\|_{2}\leq\frac{\epsilon}{4}
,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\|V{P}x\|_{2}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\|V{P}y\|_{2}-\|V{P}(y-x)\|_{2}\right)\geq\sqrt{1-\frac{2s}{p}-\epsilon}-\frac{\epsilon}{4}\sqrt{1+\frac{2s}{p}+2\epsilon}\geq 1-\frac{2s}{p}-2\epsilon.

Thus, we have

\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\geq\left(1-\frac{2s}{p}-2\epsilon\right)^{2}\geq 1-\frac{4s}{p}-4\epsilon.

Therefore, if for any
x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{\epsilon}{4}}
,
\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|<\left(\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon
, then

1-\frac{4s}{p}-4\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}\leq 1+\frac{4s}{p}+4\epsilon.

(166)

By (
164
) and (
166
),

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\text{ supp}(x)\subseteq S_{0},\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\leq\left(\frac{4s}{p}+4\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
=&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall x\in\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}},\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\leq\left(\frac{4s}{p}+4\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
\geq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall x\in\mathcal{N}_{\frac{\epsilon}{4}},\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|\leq\left(\frac{2s}{p}+\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
\geq&1-2\left(1+\frac{8}{\epsilon}\right)^{2s}\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right),\end{split}

which means

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\text{ supp}(x)\subseteq S_{0},\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|>\left(\frac{4s}{p}+4\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
\leq&2\left(1+\frac{8}{\epsilon}\right)^{2s}\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right).\end{split}

Therefore,

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\|x\|_{0}\leq 2s,\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|<\left(\frac{4s}{p}+4\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
=&1-{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists S_{0}\subset\{1,\dots,p\},|S_{0}|=2s,x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\text{ supp}(x)\subseteq S_{0},\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|>\left(\frac{4s}{p}+4\epsilon\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
\geq&1-\binom{p}{2s}\cdot 2\left(1+\frac{8}{\epsilon}\right)^{2s}\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right)\\
\geq&1-2\left(\frac{ep}{2s}\right)^{2s}\left(1+\frac{8}{\epsilon}\right)^{2s}\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)\right)\\
=&1-2\exp\left(-n\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{6}\right)+2s\cdot\log\left(\frac{ep}{2s}\right)+2s\log\left(1+\frac{8}{\epsilon}\right)\right).\end{split}

Set
\epsilon=\frac{1}{160}
, since
n\geq Cs\log p
,
\exists c>0
such that

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\|x\|_{0}\leq 2s,\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|<\left(\frac{4s}{p}+\frac{1}{40}\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)\geq 1-2e^{-cn}.\end{split}

Choose
\epsilon=\frac{1}{160}
, for sufficient large
n
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{p},\|x\|_{0}\leq s,\left|\frac{1}{n}\|V{P}x\|_{2}^{2}-\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right|<\frac{1}{40}\|x\|_{2}^{2}\right)>\frac{2}{3}.

Proof of Lemma
B.10
.
First, we bound the variance of
W_{ij}
. By the sub-exponential tail assumption
14
,

\begin{split}\var(W_{ij})=&\int_{0}^{\infty}{\mathrm{pr}}\{(W_{ij}-\nu_{ij})^{2}\geq t\}dt=\int_{0}^{\infty}Ce^{-c(t/\nu_{ij})\wedge\sqrt{t}}dt\\
\leq&\int_{0}^{\infty}Ce^{-ct/\nu_{ij}}dt+\int_{\nu_{ij}^{2}}^{\infty}Ce^{-c\sqrt{t}}dt\leq C\nu_{ij}/c+\int_{\nu_{ij}^{2}}^{\infty}Ct^{-2}dt\leq C\nu_{ij}.\end{split}

(167)

Therefore, we know that
F\leq C
for some constant
C
.
Let

f(t)=\log(t+\frac{1}{2})-\log\nu_{ij}-\frac{t-\nu_{ij}+\frac{1}{2}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{(t-\nu_{ij}+\frac{1}{2})^{2}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}.

(
167
) and the similar proof of (
79
) together show that for
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{\epsilon}
,

\left|Ef(W_{ij})\right|\leq C\nu_{ij}^{-3/2+\epsilon}.

Recall that
E(W_{0})=v
, we have

E\{f(W_{ij})\}=E\{\log(W_{ij}+1/2)\}-\log\nu_{ij}-\frac{1}{2\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\var(W_{ij})+\frac{1}{4}}{2\nu_{ij}^{2}}=E\{\log(W_{ij}+1/2)\}-\log\nu_{ij}+\frac{R_{ij}-1}{2\nu_{ij}}+\frac{1}{8\nu_{ij}^{2}},

where
R_{ij}=\frac{\var(W_{ij})}{\nu_{ij}}
and
|R_{ij}-1|\leq F
.
(
167
) and the previous two inequalities together imply that

\left|E\{\log(W_{ij}+1/2)\}-\log\nu_{ij}\right|\leq\frac{F}{2\nu_{ij}}+\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}^{3/2-\epsilon}}

for all
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{\epsilon}
.

By the sub-exponential tail assumption
14
, we know that for any
0\leq t\leq 9\nu_{ij}
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(|W_{ij}^{\prime}-\nu_{ij}|\geq t\right)\leq{\mathrm{pr}}\left(|W_{ij}-\nu_{ij}|\geq t\right)+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(W_{ij}\neq W_{ij}^{\prime}\right)\leq C\exp\left(-ct^{2}/\nu_{ij}\right)+C\exp\left(-c\nu_{ij}\right)\leq C\exp\left(-ct^{2}/\nu_{ij}\right).

Moreover, for any
t>9\nu_{ij}
, by the definition of
W_{ij}^{\prime}
, we have
{\mathbb{P}}\left(|W_{ij}^{\prime}-\nu_{ij}|\geq t\right)=0
. Therefore, for any
t\geq 0
, we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(|W_{ij}^{\prime}-\nu_{ij}|\geq t\right)\leq C\exp\left(-ct^{2}/\nu_{ij}\right).

(168)

Similar arguments of (
83
) lead us to

\left|E\left\{\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}-\log\left\{\left(W_{ij}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}\right|\leq\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}^{3/2-\epsilon}}.

Therefore, we conclude that for any
\nu_{ij}\geq C_{\epsilon}
,

\left|E\left\{\log\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}-\log\nu_{ij}\right|\leq\frac{F}{2\nu_{ij}}+\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}^{3/2-\epsilon}}.

Proof of Lemma
B.11
.
Let
g(t)=\log^{2}(t+\frac{1}{2})-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}-2\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}(t-\nu_{ij}+\frac{1}{2})-\frac{1-\log\nu_{ij}}{v^{2}}(t-\nu_{ij}+\frac{1}{2})^{2}
. Then

\begin{split}E\{g(W_{ij})\}=&E\left\{\log^{2}\left(W_{ij}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}-\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}-1}{\nu_{ij}^{2}}\left\{\var(W_{ij})+\frac{1}{4}\right\}\\
=&E\left\{\log^{2}\left(W_{ij}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}+(R_{\nu_{ij}}-1)\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}-\frac{R_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{\log\nu_{ij}-1}{4\nu_{ij}^{2}},\end{split}

where
R_{ij}=\frac{\var(W_{ij})}{\nu_{ij}}
. By similar arguments of (
88
) and (
90
), one has

\left|E\{g(W_{ij})\}\right|\leq C/\nu_{ij}

and

\left|E\left\{\log^{2}\left(W_{ij}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}-E\left\{\log^{2}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}\right|\leq\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}}.

for any
v\geq C
.
(
167
) and the precious inequalities together show that

\left|E\left\{\log^{2}\left(W_{ij}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}-\log^{2}\nu_{ij}\right|\leq F\frac{\log\nu_{ij}}{\nu_{ij}}+\frac{C}{\nu_{ij}}

provided that
\nu\geq C
.

Proof of Lemma
B.12
.
By Lemma
B.10
and (
168
), we can prove Lemma
B.12
essentially the same as the proof of Lemma
B.3
.

Proof of Lemma
B.13
.
By the same proof of (
98
), we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left\{\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\varepsilon_{k}\right|>t\Big|W^{\prime}_{kj},1\leq k\leq n,1\leq j\leq p\right\}\leq 2e^{-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}^{2}}}.

(169)

Denote
D_{ki}=\phi(W_{ki})-E\phi(W_{ki}),Q_{ki}=D_{ki}^{2}-ED_{ki}^{2},M_{ki}=\left(\bar{{B}}_{W}\right)_{ki}-E\left(\bar{{B}}_{W}\right)_{ki}
,
R_{ki}=M_{ki}^{2}-EM_{ki}^{2}
.
By the same proof of (
105
), we have

\begin{split}{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\varepsilon\|_{\infty}>t\right)\leq Cp\left(e^{-\frac{ct^{2}}{n\sigma^{2}}}+e^{-cn}\right).\end{split}

(170)

By Lemma
B.10
and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}&\left(\sum_{j\in S}E\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{j\in S}\left(E\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)^{2}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq s\left(\frac{F^{2}}{\nu_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{C}{\nu_{\min}^{3-2\epsilon}}\right)\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2},\end{split}

which means

\left|\sum_{j\in S}E\left(\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)-\log\nu_{kj}\right)\beta_{j}^{*}\right|\leq\sqrt{s}\left(\frac{F}{\nu_{\min}}+\frac{C}{\nu_{\min}^{3/2-\epsilon}}\right)\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}.

(171)

Moreover, by Lemma
B.10
, Lemma
B.11
and
\nu_{\min}\asymp\nu_{kj}
,

\begin{split}\var\{\phi_{1}(W_{kj}^{\prime})\}=&E\{\phi_{1}^{2}(W_{kj}^{\prime})\}-[E\{\phi_{1}(W_{kj}^{\prime})\}]^{2}\\
\leq&\log^{2}(\nu_{ki})+F\frac{\log(\nu_{kj})}{\nu_{kj}}+\frac{C}{\nu_{kj}}-\left\{\log(\nu_{kj})-\frac{F}{\nu_{kj}}-\frac{C}{\nu_{kj}^{3/2-\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}\\
\leq&C\left(F\frac{\log(\nu_{\min})}{\nu_{\min}}+\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}\right).\end{split}

Therefore, we have

\begin{split}&\var\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right\}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\var\left\{\left(\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}\right)_{ki}\right\}\\
=&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{p}\right)^{2}\var\left\{\phi_{1}(W_{ki}^{\prime})\right\}+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{j\neq i}\var\left\{\phi_{1}(W_{kj}^{\prime})\right\}\right]\leq Cn\left(F\frac{\log(\nu_{\min})}{\nu_{\min}}+\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}\right).\end{split}

and

\var\left(\sum_{j\in S}U_{kj}\beta_{j}^{*}\right)=\sum_{j\in S}\beta_{j}^{*2}\var\left(U_{kj}\right)=\sum_{j\in S}\beta_{j}^{*2}\var\left\{\phi_{1}\left(W^{\prime}_{kj}\right)\right\}\leq C\left(F\frac{\log(\nu_{\min})}{\nu_{\min}}+\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}\right)\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}.

Similarly to (
111
), we can show that

\begin{split}\left|E\left[{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\right]_{i}\right|\leq Cn\sqrt{s}\left(\frac{F}{\nu_{\min}}+\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}^{3/2-\epsilon}}\right)\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}+C\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{\nu_{\min}}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}.\end{split}

(172)

By the similar proof of (
112
), we have

{\mathrm{pr}}\left[\|{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\|_{\infty}\geq C\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}+Cn\sqrt{s}\left\{\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}+F\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right]\leq e^{-C^{\prime}\log p}.

(173)

Then similar arguments of (
113
) and (
114
) can show that

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\left(\sigma^{2}+\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+Cn^{2}s\left\{\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{3-2\epsilon}+F^{2}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{2}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left[\|{P}\left({A}_{W^{\prime}}-{B}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}V\right)\beta^{*}\|_{\infty}\geq C\sqrt{n\log p}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}+Cn\sqrt{s}\left\{\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}+F\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}\right]\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}\epsilon\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\cdot\sigma^{2}}\right)\\
\leq&3e^{-C^{\prime}\log p}=3p^{-C^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(174)

and

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{A}}_{W}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W}^{\top}y\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\left(\sigma^{2}+\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+Cn^{2}s\left\{\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{3-2\epsilon}+F^{2}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{2}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\\
\leq&{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\|\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}\beta^{*}-\bar{{B}}_{W^{\prime}}^{\top}y\|_{\infty}>C\sqrt{n\log p\left(\sigma^{2}+\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+Cn^{2}s\left\{\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{3-2\epsilon}+F^{2}\left(\frac{p}{\bar{\nu}}\right)^{2}\right\}\|\beta^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\\
&+{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists 1\leq i\leq n,1\leq j\leq p,W_{ij}\neq W^{\prime}_{ij}\right)\\
\leq&3p^{-C^{\prime}}+Cnpe^{-c\nu_{\min}}\leq 3p^{-C^{\prime}}+Cnp\exp\left(-C^{\prime\prime}\log(np)\right)\leq 4p^{-C^{\prime}},\end{split}

(175)

where the second last inequality holds since
\nu_{\min}\geq C\log(np)
.

Finally, (
167
) shows that
F^{2}\leq C
, which has finished the proof of Lemma
B.13
.

Lemma C.6
(RIP condition for general setting).

Suppose
n\geq cs\log p
and
|\log(\nu_{ij})-\log(\nu_{kl})|\leq a
for some constant
a
for all
1\leq i,k\leq n,1\leq j,l\leq p
. If
\bar{V}=V\{{I}_{p}-(1/p)1_{p}1_{p}^{\top}\}
satisfies RIP condition with constant
\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})>0
, ,
\bar{\nu}\geq Cp\{s\log s+\log(np)\}
, then

\bar{{B}}_{W}
satisfies RIP condition with constants
\delta_{2s}(\bar{{B}}_{W})=2\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})
, i.e.,

n\left(1-2\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq x^{\top}\bar{{A}}_{W}x\leq n\left(1+2\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}

(176)

holds for all
2s
-sparse vector
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
, with probability
1-2\times 9^{2s}e^{2s\log p-cn}
.

Proof of Lemma
C.6
.
By Lemmas
B.10
,
B.11
and the similar arguments of (
65
) in Lemma
C.3
,

{\mathrm{pr}}\left(\exists x,\|x\|_{0}\leq 2s,\|x\|_{2}=1,\left|x^{\top}(\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}})x\right|\geq\frac{1}{2}\delta\left(\Gamma\right)n\right)\leq e^{2s\log p}\times 9^{2s}\times 2e^{-cn}.

(177)

In addition, similarly to (
68
), we know that

\left|x^{\top}(E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-\bar{V}^{\top}\bar{V})x\right|\leq C_{1}sn\left(F\frac{\log\nu_{\min}}{\nu_{\min}}+\frac{1}{\nu_{\min}}\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}

for some constant
C_{1}>0
.
Since
\nu_{\min}\gtrsim\bar{\nu}/p\geq C(s+Fs\log s)
, we know that

\left|x^{\top}(E\bar{{A}}_{W^{\prime}}-\bar{V}^{\top}\bar{V})x\right|\leq\frac{1}{2}\delta_{2s}(\bar{V})n\|x\|_{2}^{2}.

Combining (
177
) and the previous inequality together, we know that

n\left(1-2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}\leq x^{\top}\bar{{A}}_{W}x\leq n\left(1+2\delta_{2s}\left(\bar{V}\right)\right)\|x\|_{2}^{2}

with probability
1-2\times 9^{2s}e^{2s\log p-cn}
.

Lemma C.7
.

Denote
\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}\triangleq\{x:x\in S^{p-1},\|x\|_{2}=1,\text{supp}(x)\subseteq S_{0}\}
, where
S_{0}
is a subset of
\{1,\dots,p\}
. Then
\forall p\times p
matrix
\widetilde{{A}}
,

\sup_{x\in\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}}\left|x^{\top}\widetilde{{A}}x\right|\leq(1-2\epsilon)^{-1}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}\left|x^{\top}\widetilde{{A}}x\right|,

(178)

where
\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
is an
\epsilon
-net of
\mathcal{A}_{S_{0}}
.

Proof of Lemma
C.7
.
Suppose
S_{0}=\{i_{1},\dots,i_{s_{0}}\}
, by considering the submatrix
\tilde{{A}}_{S_{0}\times S_{0}}
with
\left(\widetilde{{A}}_{S_{0}\times S_{0}}\right)_{jk}=\left(\widetilde{{A}}\right)_{i_{j},i_{k}}
it is equivalent to prove

\sup_{x\in S^{s_{0}-1}}\left|x^{\top}\tilde{{A}}_{S_{0}\times S_{0}}x\right|\leq\left(1-2\epsilon\right)^{-1}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon,s_{0}}}\left|x^{\top}\tilde{{A}}_{S_{0}\times S_{0}}x\right|,

(179)

where
\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon,s_{0}}
is an
\epsilon
-net of
S^{s_{0}-1}
.
By Lemma 5.4 in
Vershynin 2010
, (
179
) holds, therefore (
178
) holds.
